<!-- LOVABLE:BEGIN -->
> [!IMPORTANT]
> This project is connected to [Lovable](https://lovable.dev). Avoid rewriting
> published git history — force pushing, or rebasing/amending/squashing commits
> that are already pushed — as it rewrites history on Lovable's side and the
> user will likely lose their project history.
>
> Commits you push to the connected branch sync back to Lovable and show up in
> the editor, so keep the branch in a working state.
<!-- LOVABLE:END -->

- Data access uses the browser Supabase client with RLS scoped to user_id; queries live in src/lib/machines.ts as queryOptions (single source for cache keys).
- Manual PDFs go in the private `manuals` bucket under `<user_id>/<machine_id>/`, opened via signed URLs (storage RLS checks the first folder).

# Welcome to your Lovable project

This project was built with [Lovable](https://lovable.dev).

## Build with Lovable

Open your project in the [Lovable editor](https://lovable.dev) and keep building.

- **Ship faster**: describe what you want to build and Lovable handles the code.
- **Stay in sync**: connect the project to GitHub and every change made in Lovable is committed straight to your repository.
- **Full ownership**: this code is yours. Push to your repository and your changes sync back into Lovable, ready for your next prompt.

## Development

Prefer working locally? You need Node.js and npm — [install with nvm](https://github.com/nvm-sh/nvm#installing-and-updating).

```sh
git clone <this-repository-url>
cd <repository-name>
npm i
npm run dev
```

## Built with

- TanStack Start
- TypeScript
- React
- Tailwind CSS

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/roadmap.md
####################################################################################################

# Roadmap — PrediTec

- [x] 1. Visual e páginas (início, lista com busca, ficha da máquina com abas)
- [x] 2. Login, cadastro de máquinas, histórico de manutenção, envio de manuais
- [x] 3. Inspeções preditivas (vibração, temperatura, óleo…) com gráficos e alertas
- [x] 4. Sugestões de manuais por IA após a pesquisa
- [x] 5. Painel com máquinas em alerta e manutenções próximas

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/vite.config.ts
####################################################################################################

// @lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config already includes the following — do NOT add them manually
// or the app will break with duplicate plugins:
//   - TanStack devtools (dev-only, first), tanstackStart, viteReact, tailwindcss, tsConfigPaths,
//     nitro (build-only using cloudflare as a default target), VITE_* env injection, @ path alias,
//     React/TanStack dedupe, error logger plugins, and sandbox detection (port/host/strictPort).
// You can pass additional config via defineConfig({ vite: { ... }, etc... }) if needed.
import { defineConfig } from "@lovable.dev/vite-tanstack-config";

export default defineConfig({
  tanstackStart: {
    // Redirect TanStack Start's bundled server entry to src/server.ts (our SSR error wrapper).
    // nitro/vite builds from this
    server: { entry: "server" },
  },
});

    include: ["src/**/*.{test,spec}.{ts,tsx}"],
  },
  resolve: {
    alias: { "@": path.resolve(__dirname, "./src") },
  },
});

####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/drizzle/migrations/0000_migration.sql
####################################################################################################

CREATE TABLE public.machines (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  name text NOT NULL,
  manufacturer text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  model text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  serial_number text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  category text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  location text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  installed_at date,
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ok',
  specs jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  notes text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.machines TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.machines TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.machines ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own machines all" ON public.machines FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE TABLE public.maintenance_records (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  machine_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.machines(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  performed_at date NOT NULL DEFAULT current_date,
  kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'preventiva',
  technician text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  description text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  cost numeric,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.maintenance_records TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.maintenance_records TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.maintenance_records ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own maint all" ON public.maintenance_records FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE TABLE public.manuals (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  machine_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.machines(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  title text NOT NULL,
  url text,
  file_path text,
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.manuals TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.manuals TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.manuals ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own manuals all" ON public.manuals FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "manuals read own" ON storage.objects FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (bucket_id = 'manuals' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
CREATE POLICY "manuals insert own" ON storage.objects FOR INSERT TO authenticated WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'manuals' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
CREATE POLICY "manuals delete own" ON storage.objects FOR DELETE TO authenticated USING (bucket_id = 'manuals' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/drizzle/migrations/0001_migration.sql

CREATE TABLE public.inspections (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  machine_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES public.machines(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  measured_at date NOT NULL DEFAULT current_date,
  technique text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'vibracao',
  value numeric NOT NULL,
  unit text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  alert_limit numeric,
  critical_limit numeric,
  notes text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.inspections TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.inspections TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.inspections ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own inspections all" ON public.inspections FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/drizzle/migrations/0007_photos_checklists.sql
####################################################################################################

ALTER TABLE public.maintenance_records ADD COLUMN photos jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb;
ALTER TABLE public.maintenance_records ADD COLUMN checklist jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb;

CREATE TABLE public.checklist_templates (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  kind text NOT NULL,
  items jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
  UNIQUE (user_id, kind)
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.checklist_templates TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.checklist_templates TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.checklist_templates ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own templates all" ON public.checklist_templates FOR ALL TO authenticated
  USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

CREATE POLICY "own maint photos select" ON storage.objects FOR SELECT TO authenticated
  USING (bucket_id = 'maintenance-photos' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
CREATE POLICY "own maint photos insert" ON storage.objects FOR INSERT TO authenticated
  WITH CHECK (bucket_id = 'maintenance-photos' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
CREATE POLICY "own maint photos delete" ON storage.objects FOR DELETE TO authenticated
  USING (bucket_id = 'maintenance-photos' AND (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/drizzle/migrations/0008_parts.sql
####################################################################################################

CREATE TABLE public.parts (
  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid NOT NULL DEFAULT auth.uid(),
  machine_id uuid REFERENCES public.machines(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
  name text NOT NULL,
  quantity integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'quebrada',
  notes text NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()
);
GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.parts TO authenticated;
GRANT ALL ON public.parts TO service_role;
ALTER TABLE public.parts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY "own parts all" ON public.parts FOR ALL TO authenticated USING (auth.uid() = user_id) WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);

ALTER TABLE public.parts ADD COLUMN supplier text NOT NULL DEFAULT '';
ALTER TABLE public.parts ADD COLUMN unit_price numeric;
ALTER TABLE public.parts ADD COLUMN purchased_at date;
ALTER TABLE public.parts ADD COLUMN maintenance_id uuid REFERENCES public.maintenance_records(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { routeTree } from "./routeTree.gen";

export const getRouter = () => {
  const queryClient = new QueryClient();

  const router = createRouter({
    routeTree,
    context: { queryClient },
    scrollRestoration: true,
    defaultPreloadStaleTime: 0,
  });

  return router;
};


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/server.ts
####################################################################################################

import "./lib/error-capture";

import { consumeLastCapturedError } from "./lib/error-capture";
import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";

type ServerEntry = {
  fetch: (request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) => Promise<Response> | Response;
};

let serverEntryPromise: Promise<ServerEntry> | undefined;

async function getServerEntry(): Promise<ServerEntry> {
  if (!serverEntryPromise) {
    serverEntryPromise = import("@tanstack/react-start/server-entry").then(
      (m) => (m.default ?? m) as ServerEntry,
    );
  }
  return serverEntryPromise;
}

// h3 swallows in-handler throws into a normal 500 Response with body
// {"unhandled":true,"message":"HTTPError"} — try/catch alone never fires for those.
async function normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response: Response): Promise<Response> {
  if (response.status < 500) return response;
  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";
  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) return response;

  const body = await response.clone().text();
  if (!isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body)) return response;

  console.error(consumeLastCapturedError() ?? new Error(`h3 swallowed SSR error: ${body}`));
  return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
    status: 500,
    headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
  });
}

function isH3SwallowedErrorBody(body: string): boolean {
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(body) as { unhandled?: unknown; message?: unknown };
    return payload.unhandled === true && payload.message === "HTTPError";
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export default {
  async fetch(request: Request, env: unknown, ctx: unknown) {
    try {
      const handler = await getServerEntry();
      const response = await handler.fetch(request, env, ctx);
      return await normalizeCatastrophicSsrResponse(response);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
        status: 500,
        headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
      });
    }
  },
};


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/start.ts
####################################################################################################

import { createStart, createCsrfMiddleware, createMiddleware } from "@tanstack/react-start";

import { renderErrorPage } from "./lib/error-page";
import { attachSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-attacher";

const errorMiddleware = createMiddleware().server(async ({ next }) => {
  try {
    return await next();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error != null && typeof error === "object" && "statusCode" in error) {
      throw error;
    }
    console.error(error);
    return new Response(renderErrorPage(), {
      status: 500,
      headers: { "content-type": "text/html; charset=utf-8" },
    });
  }
});

// Start installs this automatically when src/start.ts is absent; defining the
// file opts out, so re-add it explicitly to keep server functions protected
// from cross-site requests.
const csrfMiddleware = createCsrfMiddleware({
  filter: (ctx) => ctx.handlerType === "serverFn",
});

export const startInstance = createStart(() => ({
  functionMiddleware: [attachSupabaseAuth],
  requestMiddleware: [errorMiddleware, csrfMiddleware],
}));

/*
 * Design system definition.
 *
 * The @theme inline block maps CSS custom properties to Tailwind utility
 * classes (e.g. --color-primary -> bg-primary, text-primary).
 *
 * The :root and .dark blocks define the actual color values using oklch.
 * All colors MUST use oklch format.
 *
 * To add a new semantic color:
 * 1. Add the variable to :root (light value) and .dark (dark value)
 * 2. Register it in @theme inline as --color-<name>: var(--<name>)
 */

}

  font-family: var(--font-mono-ui);
}

@utility bg-blueprint {
  background-image:
    linear-gradient(var(--grid-line) 1px, transparent 1px),
    linear-gradient(90deg, var(--grid-line) 1px, transparent 1px);

}

  font-size: 0.7rem;
  letter-spacing: 0.12em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
  color: var(--muted-foreground);
}

import { Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { LogOut } from "lucide-react";
import logo from "@/assets/maqsense-logo.png";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { MaintenanceBell } from "@/components/MaintenanceBell";

export function Logo() {
  return (
    <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 font-bold tracking-tight">
      <img src={logo} alt="" width={32} height={32} className="size-8 rounded-sm" />
      MaqSense
    </Link>
  );
}

export function AppHeader() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  async function signOut() {
    await qc.cancelQueries();
    qc.clear();
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    navigate({ to: "/auth", replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <header className="border-b bg-background/80 backdrop-blur">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 max-w-6xl items-center justify-between px-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <Logo />
          <nav className="flex gap-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <Link to="/painel" activeProps={{ className: "text-foreground" }}>
              Painel
            </Link>
            <Link to="/checklists" activeProps={{ className: "text-foreground" }}>
              Checklists
            </Link>
            <Link to="/pecas" activeProps={{ className: "text-foreground" }}>
              Peças
            </Link>
            <Link to="/maquinas" activeOptions={{ exact: true }} activeProps={{ className: "text-foreground" }}>
              Máquinas
            </Link>
            <Link to="/maquinas/nova" activeProps={{ className: "text-foreground" }}>
              Cadastrar
            </Link>
          </nav>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1">
          <MaintenanceBell />
          <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={signOut}>
            <LogOut /> Sair
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/ChecklistTemplatesEditor.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useState } from "react";
import { useQueryClient, useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { MAINTENANCE_KINDS, checklistTemplatesQuery, templateItems } from "@/lib/machines";

export function ChecklistTemplatesEditor() {
  const { data: ts } = useSuspenseQuery(checklistTemplatesQuery());
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<string>("preventiva");
  const items = templateItems(ts, kind);
  const [text, setText] = useState("");

  async function save(next: string[]) {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("checklist_templates").upsert({ kind, items: next, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }, { onConflict: "user_id,kind" });
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível salvar o checklist.");
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["checklist-templates"] });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
      <p className="label-tech">Checklists por tipo de manutenção</p>
      <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
        {MAINTENANCE_KINDS.map((k) => (
          <button type="button" key={k} onClick={() => setKind(k)}
            className={`flex-1 rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-xs capitalize ${kind === k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>{k}</button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {items.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Nenhum item ainda para {kind}.</p>}
      {items.map((it, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
          <span>{it}</span>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover item" onClick={() => save(items.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}><X /></Button>
        </div>
      ))}
      <form className="flex gap-2" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (text.trim()) { save([...items, text.trim()]); setText(""); } }}>
        <Input placeholder="Novo item (ex.: Verificar nível de óleo)" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
        <Button type="submit" size="icon" variant="outline" aria-label="Adicionar item"><Plus /></Button>
      </form>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Vale para todas as máquinas. Aparece ao concluir uma manutenção desse tipo.</p>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/CompleteMaintenanceDialog.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { MAINTENANCE_KINDS, afterCompletion, buildChecklist, checklistTemplatesQuery, templateItems, uploadMaintenancePhotos, type Machine } from "@/lib/machines";

type M = Pick<Machine, "id" | "name" | "next_maintenance_task" | "maintenance_interval_days">;

export function CompleteMaintenanceDialog({ machine, children }: { machine: M; children: ReactNode }) {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const { data: ts = [] } = useQuery(checklistTemplatesQuery());
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<string>("preventiva");
  const items = templateItems(ts, kind);
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [before, setBefore] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [after, setAfter] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [f, setF] = useState({ performed_at: new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-CA"), technician: "", notes: "", parts_used: "", downtime: "" });

  async function save(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    const task = machine.next_maintenance_task?.trim();
    const description = [task, f.notes.trim()].filter(Boolean).join(" — ");
    const { data: rec, error } = await supabase.from("maintenance_records").insert({
      machine_id: machine.id, performed_at: f.performed_at, kind,
      technician: f.technician, description, parts_used: f.parts_used,
      downtime_hours: f.downtime ? Number(f.downtime) : null,
      checklist: buildChecklist(items, checked),
    }).select("id").single();
    if (rec && (before.length || after.length)) {
      const photos = await uploadMaintenancePhotos(machine.id, rec.id, [
        ...before.map((file) => ({ file, phase: "antes" as const })),
        ...after.map((file) => ({ file, phase: "depois" as const })),
      ]);
      await supabase.from("maintenance_records").update({ photos }).eq("id", rec.id);
    }
    const patch = afterCompletion(machine.maintenance_interval_days, f.performed_at);
    const { error: e2 } = error ? { error } : await supabase.from("machines").update(patch).eq("id", machine.id);
    setBusy(false);
    if (error || e2) return void toast.error("Não foi possível salvar.");
    await Promise.all([
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machines"] }),
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machine", machine.id] }),
      qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["maintenance", machine.id] }),
    ]);
    setOpen(false);
    toast.success("next_maintenance_at" in patch
      ? `Manutenção concluída. Próxima em ${new Date(patch.next_maintenance_at + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}.`
      : "Manutenção concluída e registrada no histórico.");
  }

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>{children}</DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent className="max-h-[90vh] overflow-y-auto">
        <DialogHeader><DialogTitle>Concluir manutenção — {machine.name}</DialogTitle></DialogHeader>
        {machine.next_maintenance_task && <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{machine.next_maintenance_task}</p>}
        <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-3">
          <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-date">Data da conclusão</Label>
            <Input id="cm-date" type="date" required value={f.performed_at} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, performed_at: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
            {MAINTENANCE_KINDS.map((k) => (
              <button type="button" key={k} onClick={() => { setKind(k); setChecked(new Set()); }}
                className={`flex-1 rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-xs capitalize ${kind === k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>{k}</button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5 rounded-sm border p-3">
            <Label>Checklist ({checked.size}/{items.length})</Label>
            {items.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Sem checklist para {kind}. Crie um na aba Histórico.</p>}
            {items.map((it) => (
              <label key={it} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <Checkbox checked={checked.has(it)} onCheckedChange={(c) => setChecked((p) => { const n = new Set(p); c ? n.add(it) : n.delete(it); return n; })} />
                {it}
              </label>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
            <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-before">Fotos antes</Label>
              <Input id="cm-before" type="file" accept="image/*" multiple onChange={(e) => setBefore([...(e.target.files ?? [])])} /></div>
            <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-after">Fotos depois</Label>
              <Input id="cm-after" type="file" accept="image/*" multiple onChange={(e) => setAfter([...(e.target.files ?? [])])} /></div>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-tech">Responsável</Label>
            <Input id="cm-tech" required value={f.technician} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, technician: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-parts">Peças utilizadas</Label>
            <Input id="cm-parts" placeholder="Ex.: 1 filtro de óleo, 4 L de óleo ISO 46" value={f.parts_used} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, parts_used: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-notes">Observações</Label>
            <Textarea id="cm-notes" value={f.notes} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, notes: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5"><Label htmlFor="cm-dt">Tempo parado (horas)</Label>
            <Input id="cm-dt" type="number" step="0.1" min="0" value={f.downtime} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, downtime: e.target.value })} /></div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            {machine.maintenance_interval_days
              ? `A próxima manutenção será marcada para ${machine.maintenance_interval_days} dias após a conclusão.`
              : "Esta máquina não tem intervalo definido; edite a máquina para repetir automaticamente."}
          </p>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>Concluir e registrar</Button>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/InspectionsTab.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useQueryClient, useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import { CartesianGrid, Line, LineChart, ReferenceLine, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from "recharts";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import {
  STATUS, TECHNIQUES, inspectionsQuery, readingLevel, suggestedStatus,
  type Machine, type TechniqueKey,
} from "@/lib/machines";

const num = (v: unknown) => (v == null ? null : Number(v));
const fmtDate = (d: string) => new Date(d + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");

export function InspectionsTab({ machine }: { machine: Machine }) {
  const { data: all } = useSuspenseQuery(inspectionsQuery(machine.id));
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [tech, setTech] = useState<TechniqueKey>("vibracao");
  const last = [...all].reverse().find((r) => r.technique === tech);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ measured_at: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), value: "", alert_limit: "", critical_limit: "", notes: "" });

  const readings = useMemo(() => all.filter((r) => r.technique === tech), [all, tech]);
  const chart = readings.map((r) => ({ date: fmtDate(r.measured_at), value: Number(r.value) }));
  const alertL = num(last?.alert_limit);
  const critL = num(last?.critical_limit);
  const suggested = suggestedStatus(all);

  async function add(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const { error } = await supabase.from("inspections").insert({
      machine_id: machine.id, technique: tech, unit: TECHNIQUES[tech].unit,
      measured_at: form.measured_at, value: Number(form.value), notes: form.notes,
      alert_limit: form.alert_limit ? Number(form.alert_limit) : alertL,
      critical_limit: form.critical_limit ? Number(form.critical_limit) : critL,
    });
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível registrar.");
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, value: "", notes: "" }));
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["inspections", machine.id] });
    toast.success("Leitura registrada.");
  }

  async function applyStatus() {
    await supabase.from("machines").update({ status: suggested }).eq("id", machine.id);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machine", machine.id] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machines"] });
    toast.success("Estado da máquina atualizado.");
  }

  async function del(id: string) {
    await supabase.from("inspections").delete().eq("id", id);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["inspections", machine.id] });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {all.length > 0 && suggested !== machine.status && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-sm border border-warning/40 bg-card p-4 text-sm">
          <span>As últimas leituras indicam o estado <b>{STATUS[suggested].label}</b>.</span>
          <Button size="sm" onClick={applyStatus}>Atualizar estado da máquina</Button>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
        {(Object.keys(TECHNIQUES) as TechniqueKey[]).map((k) => (
          <button key={k} onClick={() => setTech(k)}
            className={`rounded-sm px-3 py-1 text-sm ${tech === k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
            {TECHNIQUES[k].label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
            <p className="label-tech">Tendência · {TECHNIQUES[tech].label} ({TECHNIQUES[tech].unit})</p>
            {chart.length < 2 ? (
              <p className="py-16 text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">Registre ao menos duas leituras para ver a tendência.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="mt-4 h-64">
                <ResponsiveContainer>
                  <LineChart data={chart}>
                    <CartesianGrid stroke="var(--border)" strokeDasharray="3 3" />
                    <XAxis dataKey="date" stroke="var(--muted-foreground)" fontSize={11} />
                    <YAxis stroke="var(--muted-foreground)" fontSize={11} />
                    <Tooltip contentStyle={{ background: "var(--popover)", border: "1px solid var(--border)" }} />
                    {alertL != null && <ReferenceLine y={alertL} stroke="var(--warning)" strokeDasharray="4 4" label={{ value: "Alerta", fill: "var(--warning)", fontSize: 11 }} />}
                    {critL != null && <ReferenceLine y={critL} stroke="var(--destructive)" strokeDasharray="4 4" label={{ value: "Crítico", fill: "var(--destructive)", fontSize: 11 }} />}
                    <Line type="monotone" dataKey="value" stroke="var(--primary)" strokeWidth={2} dot={{ r: 3 }} />
                  </LineChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
            {readings.length === 0 && <p className="p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma leitura de {TECHNIQUES[tech].label.toLowerCase()}.</p>}
            {[...readings].reverse().map((r) => (
              <div key={r.id} className="flex items-center gap-4 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0">
                <span className="w-24 font-mono-ui text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fmtDate(r.measured_at)}</span>
                <span className="flex-1 font-mono-ui">{Number(r.value)} {r.unit}{r.notes && <span className="ml-3 font-sans text-sm text-muted-foreground">{r.notes}</span>}</span>
                <StatusBadge status={readingLevel({ value: Number(r.value), alert_limit: num(r.alert_limit), critical_limit: num(r.critical_limit) })} />
                <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Excluir leitura" onClick={() => del(r.id)}><Trash2 /></Button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <form onSubmit={add} className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
          <p className="label-tech">Nova leitura · {TECHNIQUES[tech].label}</p>
          <Input type="date" required value={form.measured_at} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, measured_at: e.target.value })} />
          <Input type="number" step="any" required placeholder={`Valor (${TECHNIQUES[tech].unit})`} value={form.value} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, value: e.target.value })} />
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input type="number" step="any" placeholder={alertL != null ? `Alerta: ${alertL}` : "Limite alerta"} value={form.alert_limit} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, alert_limit: e.target.value })} />
            <Input type="number" step="any" placeholder={critL != null ? `Crítico: ${critL}` : "Limite crítico"} value={form.critical_limit} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, critical_limit: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Limites em branco repetem os da última leitura.</p>
          <Input placeholder="Observação" value={form.notes} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })} />
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full">Registrar leitura</Button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/MachineForm.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useState } from "react";
import { Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { STATUS, parseSpecs, type Machine, type Spec } from "@/lib/machines";

export type MachineInput = {
  name: string;
  manufacturer: string;
  model: string;
  serial_number: string;
  category: string;
  location: string;
  installed_at: string | null;
  next_maintenance_at: string | null;
  next_maintenance_task: string;
  next_maintenance_done: boolean;
  maintenance_interval_days: number | null;
  status: string;
  notes: string;
  specs: Spec[];
};

export function MachineForm({
  initial,
  onSubmit,
  submitLabel,
}: {
  initial?: Machine;
  onSubmit: (v: MachineInput) => Promise<void>;
  submitLabel: string;
}) {
  const [v, setV] = useState<MachineInput>({
    name: initial?.name ?? "",
    manufacturer: initial?.manufacturer ?? "",
    model: initial?.model ?? "",
    serial_number: initial?.serial_number ?? "",
    category: initial?.category ?? "",
    location: initial?.location ?? "",
    installed_at: initial?.installed_at ?? null,
    next_maintenance_at: initial?.next_maintenance_at ?? null,
    next_maintenance_task: initial?.next_maintenance_task ?? "",
    next_maintenance_done: initial?.next_maintenance_done ?? false,
    maintenance_interval_days: initial?.maintenance_interval_days ?? null,
    status: initial?.status ?? "ok",
    notes: initial?.notes ?? "",
    specs: initial ? parseSpecs(initial.specs) : [{ label: "Potência", value: "" }, { label: "Rotação", value: "" }],
  });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const set = (k: keyof MachineInput, val: string) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, [k]: val }));

  const fields: [keyof MachineInput, string, string?][] = [
    ["name", "Nome *", "Ex.: Compressor de ar 01"],
    ["manufacturer", "Fabricante", "Ex.: WEG, Atlas Copco"],
    ["model", "Modelo", "Ex.: W22 IR3"],
    ["serial_number", "Nº de série"],
    ["category", "Categoria", "Ex.: Motor elétrico, Bomba"],
    ["location", "Local", "Ex.: Linha 2 — Galpão B"],
  ];

  return (
    <form
      className="space-y-8"
      onSubmit={async (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setSaving(true);
        await onSubmit({ ...v, specs: v.specs.filter((s) => s.label.trim()) });
        setSaving(false);
      }}
    >
      <section className="grid gap-4 rounded-sm border bg-card p-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <p className="label-tech md:col-span-2">Identificação</p>
        {fields.map(([k, label, ph]) => (
          <div key={k} className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor={k}>{label}</Label>
            <Input id={k} required={k === "name"} placeholder={ph} value={v[k] as string} onChange={(e) => set(k, e.target.value)} />
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="installed_at">Data de instalação</Label>
          <Input id="installed_at" type="date" value={v.installed_at ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, installed_at: e.target.value || null }))} />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="next_maintenance_at">Próxima manutenção</Label>
          <Input id="next_maintenance_at" type="date" value={v.next_maintenance_at ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, next_maintenance_at: e.target.value || null, next_maintenance_done: false }))} />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="next_maintenance_task">Qual manutenção será feita</Label>
          <Input id="next_maintenance_task" placeholder="Ex.: Troca do óleo e dos filtros" value={v.next_maintenance_task} onChange={(e) => set("next_maintenance_task", e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label htmlFor="maintenance_interval_days">Repetir a cada (dias)</Label>
          <Input id="maintenance_interval_days" type="number" min="1" placeholder="Ex.: 90 — deixe vazio se não repetir" value={v.maintenance_interval_days ?? ""}
            onChange={(e) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, maintenance_interval_days: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null }))} />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Ao concluir a manutenção, a próxima data é calculada sozinha.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5 sm:col-span-2">
          <Label>Essa manutenção já foi feita?</Label>
          <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
            {[{ k: false, t: "Ainda não" }, { k: true, t: "Sim, já foi feita" }].map((o) => (
              <button type="button" key={o.t} onClick={() => setV((p) => ({ ...p, next_maintenance_done: o.k }))}
                className={`flex-1 rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-xs ${v.next_maintenance_done === o.k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>{o.t}</button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label>Estado</Label>
          <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
            {Object.entries(STATUS).map(([k, s]) => (
              <button type="button" key={k} onClick={() => set("status", k)}
                className={`flex-1 rounded-sm px-3 py-1 text-sm ${v.status === k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                {s.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-6">
        <p className="label-tech">Especificações técnicas</p>
        {v.specs.map((s, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">
            <Input placeholder="Parâmetro" value={s.label}
              onChange={(e) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, specs: p.specs.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, label: e.target.value } : x)) }))} />
            <Input placeholder="Valor (ex.: 15 kW)" value={s.value}
              onChange={(e) => setV((p) => ({ ...p, specs: p.specs.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { ...x, value: e.target.value } : x)) }))} />
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover"
              onClick={() => setV((p) => ({ ...p, specs: p.specs.filter((_, j) => j !== i) }))}>
              <X />
            </Button>
          </div>
        ))}
        <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setV((p) => ({ ...p, specs: [...p.specs, { label: "", value: "" }] }))}>
          <Plus /> Adicionar parâmetro
        </Button>
      </section>

      <section className="space-y-1.5 rounded-sm border bg-card p-6">
        <Label htmlFor="notes" className="label-tech">Observações</Label>
        <Textarea id="notes" value={v.notes} onChange={(e) => set("notes", e.target.value)} />
      </section>

      <Button type="submit" size="lg" disabled={saving}>{submitLabel}</Button>
    </form>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/MaintenanceBell.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { CompleteMaintenanceDialog } from "@/components/CompleteMaintenanceDialog";
import { Bell, Check } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@/components/ui/popover";
import { NOTICE_DAYS, daysUntil, dueSoon, machinesQuery } from "@/lib/machines";

export function MaintenanceBell() {
  const { data: machines = [], isSuccess } = useQuery(machinesQuery());
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-CA");
  const due = dueSoon(machines, today);
  const shown = useRef("");

  // Pop-up notice whenever the set of due machines changes (e.g. after saving a date).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isSuccess) return;
    const key = due.map((m) => m.id + m.next_maintenance_at).join("|");
    if (!key || key === shown.current) return;
    shown.current = key;
    toast.warning(`${due.length} manutenção(ões) em até ${NOTICE_DAYS} dias`, {
      description: due.map((m) => `${m.name}: ${m.next_maintenance_task || "manutenção não descrita"}`).join(" · "),
      duration: 8000,
    });
  }, [isSuccess, due]);

  return (
    <Popover>
      <PopoverTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Avisos de manutenção" className="relative">
          <Bell />
          {due.length > 0 && (
            <span className="absolute -right-0.5 -top-0.5 grid size-4 place-items-center rounded-full bg-primary font-mono-ui text-[10px] text-primary-foreground">
              {due.length}
            </span>
          )}
        </Button>
      </PopoverTrigger>
      <PopoverContent align="end" className="w-80 p-0">
        <p className="border-b p-3 label-tech">Manutenções em até {NOTICE_DAYS} dias</p>
        {due.length === 0 && (
          <p className="p-3 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Nenhum prazo próximo. Para receber avisos, edite a máquina e preencha "Próxima manutenção".
          </p>
        )}
        {due.map((m) => {
          const d = daysUntil(m.next_maintenance_at!, today);
          return (
            <div key={m.id} className="border-b last:border-b-0">
              <Link to="/maquinas/$id" params={{ id: m.id }} className="flex justify-between gap-2 p-3 pb-1 text-sm hover:bg-secondary/50">
                <span>
                  {m.name}
                  <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.next_maintenance_task || "Manutenção não descrita"}</span>
                </span>
                <span className={`font-mono-ui text-xs ${d < 0 ? "text-destructive" : "text-primary"}`}>
                  {d < 0 ? `${-d} dia(s) atrasada` : d === 0 ? "hoje" : `em ${d} dia(s)`}
                </span>
              </Link>
              <CompleteMaintenanceDialog machine={m}>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="mx-2 mb-2 h-7 text-xs">
                  <Check /> Marcar como feita
                </Button>
              </CompleteMaintenanceDialog>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/MaintenancePhotos.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import type { MaintenancePhoto } from "@/lib/machines";

export function MaintenancePhotos({ photos }: { photos: MaintenancePhoto[] }) {
  const [urls, setUrls] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!photos.length) return;
    supabase.storage.from("maintenance-photos").createSignedUrls(photos.map((p) => p.path), 3600).then(({ data }) => {
      const m: Record<string, string> = {};
      data?.forEach((d) => { if (d.signedUrl && d.path) m[d.path] = d.signedUrl; });
      setUrls(m);
    });
  }, [photos.map((p) => p.path).join()]);
  if (!photos.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-4">
      {(["antes", "depois"] as const).map((ph) => {
        const ps = photos.filter((p) => p.phase === ph);
        return ps.length ? (
          <div key={ph}>
            <p className="label-tech mb-1">{ph}</p>
            <div className="flex gap-1">
              {ps.map((p) => urls[p.path] ? (
                <a key={p.path} href={urls[p.path]} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">
                  <img src={urls[p.path]} alt={`Foto ${ph}`} className="size-16 rounded-sm border object-cover" />
                </a>
              ) : <div key={p.path} className="size-16 rounded-sm border bg-secondary" />)}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : null;
      })}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/MaintenancePlanCard.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { useState } from "react";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { planMaintenance, type MaintenancePlan } from "@/lib/plan-ai.functions";

export function MaintenancePlanCard({ machineId }: { machineId: string }) {
  const run = useServerFn(planMaintenance);
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState<MaintenancePlan | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function go() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      setPlan(await run({ data: { machineId } }));
      setDone(new Set());
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Falha ao gerar o plano.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const total = plan?.steps.reduce((s, x) => s + x.minutes, 0) ?? 0;

  return (
    <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="label-tech">Plano rápido da IA</p>
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={go} disabled={busy}>
          <Sparkles /> {busy ? "Montando…" : plan ? "Gerar de novo" : "Gerar checklist"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      {!plan && !busy && (
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Passo a passo, peças para separar antes e dicas para a máquina ficar menos tempo parada.
        </p>
      )}
      {plan && (
        <div className="mt-4 space-y-5">
          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium">Separar antes da parada</p>
            <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm">
              {plan.parts.map((p, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="flex justify-between border-b pb-1 last:border-b-0">
                  <span>{p.name}</span><span className="font-mono-ui text-xs text-muted-foreground">{p.qty}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            {plan.tools.length > 0 && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Ferramentas: {plan.tools.join(", ")}</p>
            )}
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium">
              Checklist <span className="font-mono-ui text-xs text-muted-foreground">≈ {total} min · {done.size}/{plan.steps.length}</span>
            </p>
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {plan.steps.map((s, i) => (
                <label key={i} className="flex cursor-pointer gap-2 rounded-sm p-2 text-sm hover:bg-secondary/50">
                  <input type="checkbox" className="mt-1 accent-primary" checked={done.has(i)}
                    onChange={() => setDone((d) => { const n = new Set(d); n.has(i) ? n.delete(i) : n.add(i); return n; })} />
                  <span className={done.has(i) ? "text-muted-foreground line-through" : ""}>
                    <span className="font-medium">{s.title}</span> <span className="font-mono-ui text-xs text-primary">{s.minutes} min</span>
                    <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.detail}</span>
                  </span>
                </label>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
          <div>
            <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium">Dicas para ganhar tempo</p>
            <ul className="list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {plan.tips.map((t, i) => <li key={i}>{t}</li>)}
            </ul>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { STATUS, type StatusKey } from "@/lib/machines";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const toneClass = {
  success: "border-success/40 text-success",
  warning: "border-warning/40 text-warning",
  destructive: "border-destructive/40 text-destructive",
};

export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const s = STATUS[(status as StatusKey) in STATUS ? (status as StatusKey) : "ok"];
  return (
    <span className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-sm border px-2 py-0.5 font-mono-ui text-xs", toneClass[s.tone])}>
      <span className="size-1.5 rounded-full bg-current" />
      {s.label}
    </span>
  );
}

import * as React from "react";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm [&>svg+div]:translate-y-[-3px] [&>svg]:absolute [&>svg]:left-4 [&>svg]:top-4 [&>svg]:text-foreground [&>svg~*]:pl-7",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-background text-foreground",
        destructive:
          "border-destructive/50 text-destructive dark:border-destructive [&>svg]:text-destructive",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  },
);

const Alert = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>
>(({ className, variant, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div ref={ref} role="alert" className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />
));
Alert.displayName = "Alert";

const AlertTitle = React.forwardRef<HTMLParagraphElement, React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement>>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <h5
      ref={ref}
      className={cn("mb-1 font-medium leading-none tracking-tight", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  ),
);
AlertTitle.displayName = "AlertTitle";

const AlertDescription = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div ref={ref} className={cn("text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed", className)} {...props} />
));
AlertDescription.displayName = "AlertDescription";

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription };

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/components/ui/breadcrumb.tsx
####################################################################################################

import * as React from "react";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { ChevronRight, MoreHorizontal } from "lucide-react";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const Breadcrumb = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"nav"> & {
    separator?: React.ReactNode;
  }
>(({ ...props }, ref) => <nav ref={ref} aria-label="breadcrumb" {...props} />);
Breadcrumb.displayName = "Breadcrumb";

const BreadcrumbList = React.forwardRef<HTMLOListElement, React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"ol">>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <ol
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 break-words text-sm text-muted-foreground sm:gap-2.5",
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  ),
);
BreadcrumbList.displayName = "BreadcrumbList";

const BreadcrumbItem = React.forwardRef<HTMLLIElement, React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"li">>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <li ref={ref} className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5", className)} {...props} />
  ),
);
BreadcrumbItem.displayName = "BreadcrumbItem";

const BreadcrumbLink = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLAnchorElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"a"> & {
    asChild?: boolean;
  }
>(({ asChild, className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "a";

  return (
    <Comp
      ref={ref}
      className={cn("transition-colors hover:text-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
BreadcrumbLink.displayName = "BreadcrumbLink";

const BreadcrumbPage = React.forwardRef<HTMLSpanElement, React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"span">>(
  ({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
    <span
      ref={ref}
      role="link"
      aria-disabled="true"
      aria-current="page"
      className={cn("font-normal text-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  ),
);
BreadcrumbPage.displayName = "BreadcrumbPage";

const BreadcrumbSeparator = ({ children, className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"li">) => (
  <li
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("[&>svg]:w-3.5 [&>svg]:h-3.5", className)}
    {...props}
  >
    {children ?? <ChevronRight />}
  </li>
);
BreadcrumbSeparator.displayName = "BreadcrumbSeparator";

const BreadcrumbEllipsis = ({ className, ...props }: React.ComponentProps<"span">) => (
  <span
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center", className)}
    {...props}
  >
    <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
    <span className="sr-only">More</span>
  </span>
);
BreadcrumbEllipsis.displayName = "BreadcrumbElipssis";

export {
  Breadcrumb,
  BreadcrumbList,
  BreadcrumbItem,
  BreadcrumbLink,
  BreadcrumbPage,
  BreadcrumbSeparator,
  BreadcrumbEllipsis,
};

import * as React from "react";
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot";
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium cursor-pointer transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow hover:bg-primary/90",
        destructive: "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-destructive/90",
        outline:
          "border border-input bg-background shadow-sm hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        secondary: "bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground shadow-sm hover:bg-secondary/80",
        ghost: "hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  },
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>, VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button";
    return (
      <Comp className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))} ref={ref} {...props} />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = "Button";

export { Button, buttonVariants };

import * as React from "react";
import { ChevronDownIcon, ChevronLeftIcon, ChevronRightIcon } from "lucide-react";
import { DayButton, DayPicker, getDefaultClassNames } from "react-day-picker";

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { Button, buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button";

function Calendar({
  className,
  classNames,
  showOutsideDays = true,
  captionLayout = "label",
  buttonVariant = "ghost",
  formatters,
  components,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayPicker> & {
  buttonVariant?: React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>["variant"];
}) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames();

  return (
    <DayPicker
      showOutsideDays={showOutsideDays}
      className={cn(
        "bg-background group/calendar p-3 [--cell-size:2rem] [[data-slot=card-content]_&]:bg-transparent [[data-slot=popover-content]_&]:bg-transparent",
        String.raw`rtl:**:[.rdp-button\_next>svg]:rotate-180`,
        String.raw`rtl:**:[.rdp-button\_previous>svg]:rotate-180`,
        className,
      )}
      captionLayout={captionLayout}
      formatters={{
        formatMonthDropdown: (date) => date.toLocaleString("default", { month: "short" }),
        ...formatters,
      }}
      classNames={{
        root: cn("w-fit", defaultClassNames.root),
        months: cn("relative flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row", defaultClassNames.months),
        month: cn("flex w-full flex-col gap-4", defaultClassNames.month),
        nav: cn(
          "absolute inset-x-0 top-0 flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-1",
          defaultClassNames.nav,
        ),
        button_previous: cn(
          buttonVariants({ variant: buttonVariant }),
          "h-(--cell-size) w-(--cell-size) select-none p-0 aria-disabled:opacity-50",
          defaultClassNames.button_previous,
        ),
        button_next: cn(
          buttonVariants({ variant: buttonVariant }),
          "h-(--cell-size) w-(--cell-size) select-none p-0 aria-disabled:opacity-50",
          defaultClassNames.button_next,
        ),
        month_caption: cn(
          "flex h-(--cell-size) w-full items-center justify-center px-(--cell-size)",
          defaultClassNames.month_caption,
        ),
        dropdowns: cn(
          "flex h-(--cell-size) w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-medium",
          defaultClassNames.dropdowns,
        ),
        dropdown_root: cn(
          "has-focus:border-ring border-input shadow-xs has-focus:ring-ring/50 has-focus:ring-[3px] relative rounded-md border",
          defaultClassNames.dropdown_root,
        ),
        dropdown: cn("bg-popover absolute inset-0 opacity-0", defaultClassNames.dropdown),
        caption_label: cn(
          "select-none font-medium",
          captionLayout === "label"
            ? "text-sm"
            : "[&>svg]:text-muted-foreground flex h-8 items-center gap-1 rounded-md pl-2 pr-1 text-sm [&>svg]:size-3.5",
          defaultClassNames.caption_label,
        ),
        table: "w-full border-collapse",
        weekdays: cn("flex", defaultClassNames.weekdays),
        weekday: cn(
          "text-muted-foreground flex-1 select-none rounded-md text-[0.8rem] font-normal",
          defaultClassNames.weekday,
        ),
        week: cn("mt-2 flex w-full", defaultClassNames.week),
        week_number_header: cn("w-(--cell-size) select-none", defaultClassNames.week_number_header),
        week_number: cn(
          "text-muted-foreground select-none text-[0.8rem]",
          defaultClassNames.week_number,
        ),
        day: cn(
          "group/day relative aspect-square h-full w-full select-none p-0 text-center [&:first-child[data-selected=true]_button]:rounded-l-md [&:last-child[data-selected=true]_button]:rounded-r-md",
          defaultClassNames.day,
        ),
        range_start: cn("bg-accent rounded-l-md", defaultClassNames.range_start),
        range_middle: cn("rounded-none", defaultClassNames.range_middle),
        range_end: cn("bg-accent rounded-r-md", defaultClassNames.range_end),
        today: cn(
          "bg-accent text-accent-foreground rounded-md data-[selected=true]:rounded-none",
          defaultClassNames.today,
        ),
        outside: cn(
          "text-muted-foreground aria-selected:text-muted-foreground",
          defaultClassNames.outside,
        ),
        disabled: cn("text-muted-foreground opacity-50", defaultClassNames.disabled),
        hidden: cn("invisible", defaultClassNames.hidden),
        ...classNames,
      }}
      components={{
        Root: ({ className, rootRef, ...props }) => {
          return <div data-slot="calendar" ref={rootRef} className={cn(className)} {...props} />;
        },
        Chevron: ({ className, orientation, ...props }) => {
          if (orientation === "left") {
            return <ChevronLeftIcon className={cn("size-4", className)} {...props} />;
          }

          if (orientation === "right") {
            return <ChevronRightIcon className={cn("size-4", className)} {...props} />;
          }

          return <ChevronDownIcon className={cn("size-4", className)} {...props} />;
        },
        DayButton: CalendarDayButton,
        WeekNumber: ({ children, ...props }) => {
          return (
            <td {...props}>
              <div className="flex size-(--cell-size) items-center justify-center text-center">
                {children}
              </div>
            </td>
          );
        },
        ...components,
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function CalendarDayButton({
  className,
  day,
  modifiers,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayButton>) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames();

  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (modifiers["focused"]) ref.current?.focus();
  }, [modifiers]);

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon"
      data-day={day.date.toLocaleDateString()}
      data-selected-single={
        modifiers["selected"] &&
        !modifiers["range_start"] &&
        !modifiers["range_end"] &&
        !modifiers["range_middle"]
      }
      data-range-start={modifiers["range_start"]}
      data-range-end={modifiers["range_end"]}
      data-range-middle={modifiers["range_middle"]}
      className={cn(
        "data-[selected-single=true]:bg-primary data-[selected-single=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-middle=true]:bg-accent data-[range-middle=true]:text-accent-foreground data-[range-start=true]:bg-primary data-[range-start=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-end=true]:bg-primary data-[range-end=true]:text-primary-foreground group-data-[focused=true]/day:border-ring group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-ring/50 flex aspect-square h-auto w-full min-w-(--cell-size) flex-col gap-1 font-normal leading-none data-[range-end=true]:rounded-md data-[range-middle=true]:rounded-none data-[range-start=true]:rounded-md group-data-[focused=true]/day:relative group-data-[focused=true]/day:z-10 group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-[3px] [&>span]:text-xs [&>span]:opacity-70",
        defaultClassNames.day,
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export { Calendar, CalendarDayButton };
