export function renderErrorPage(): string {
  return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>This page didn't load</title>
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
    <style>
      body { font: 15px/1.5 system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif; background: #fafafa; color: #111; display: grid; place-items: center; min-height: 100vh; margin: 0; padding: 1.5rem; }
      .card { max-width: 28rem; width: 100%; text-align: center; padding: 2rem; }
      h1 { font-size: 1.25rem; margin: 0 0 0.5rem; }
      p { color: #4b5563; margin: 0 0 1.5rem; }
      .actions { display: flex; gap: 0.5rem; justify-content: center; flex-wrap: wrap; }
      a, button { padding: 0.5rem 1rem; border-radius: 0.375rem; font: inherit; cursor: pointer; text-decoration: none; border: 1px solid transparent; }
      .primary { background: #111; color: #fff; }
      .secondary { background: #fff; color: #111; border-color: #d1d5db; }
    </style>
  </head>
  <body>
    <div class="card">
      <h1>This page didn't load</h1>
      <p>Something went wrong on our end. You can try refreshing or head back home.</p>
      <div class="actions">
        <button class="primary" onclick="location.reload()">Try again</button>
        <a class="secondary" href="/">Go home</a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </body>
</html>`;
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/lib/lovable-error-reporting.ts
####################################################################################################

type LovableErrorOptions = {
  mechanism?: "manual" | "onerror" | "unhandledrejection" | "react_error_boundary";
  handled?: boolean;
  severity?: "error" | "warning" | "info";
};

type LovableEvents = {
  track?: (event: string, properties?: Record<string, unknown>) => string | null;
  captureException?: (
    error: unknown,
    context?: Record<string, unknown>,
    options?: LovableErrorOptions,
  ) => void;
};

declare global {
  interface Window {
    __lovableEvents?: LovableEvents;
    __lovableReportRuntimeError?: (payload: {
      message: string;
      stack?: string;
      filename?: string;
    }) => void;
  }
}

export function reportLovableError(error: unknown, context: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
  window.__lovableEvents?.captureException?.(
    error,
    {
      source: "react_error_boundary",
      route: window.location.pathname,
      ...context,
    },
    {
      mechanism: "react_error_boundary",
      handled: false,
      severity: "error",
    },
  );
  // Prod React does not rethrow boundary-caught errors to window.onerror, so the
  // editor's telemetry never sees them. Forward to lovable.js's reporting hook,
  // which is present only inside the editor preview.
  const stack = error instanceof Error ? error.stack : undefined;
  window.__lovableReportRuntimeError?.({
    message: describeThrown(error),
    ...(stack !== undefined && { stack }),
    filename: window.location.pathname,
  });
}

const MAX_SERIALIZED_LENGTH = 2000;

// Loaders and server fns throw raw Responses and plain objects (a Supabase
// `{ message, code, details }`), which String() reduces to "[object ...]".
function describeThrown(error: unknown): string {
  if (error instanceof Response) {
    return `Response ${error.status}${error.url ? ` at ${error.url}` : ""}`;
  }
  if (error instanceof Error) return error.message;
  if (typeof error === "string") return error;
  const { message } = (error ?? {}) as { message?: unknown };
  if (typeof message === "string" && message.length > 0) return message;
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(error)?.slice(0, MAX_SERIALIZED_LENGTH) ?? String(error);
  } catch {
    return String(error);
  }
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { readingLevel, suggestedStatus } from "./machines";

describe("alertas de inspeção", () => {
  it("classifica pelo limite de alerta e crítico", () => {
    expect(readingLevel({ value: 2, alert_limit: 4.5, critical_limit: 7.1 })).toBe("ok");
    expect(readingLevel({ value: 4.5, alert_limit: 4.5, critical_limit: 7.1 })).toBe("atencao");

  });

    const base = { id: "", user_id: "", machine_id: "", unit: "", notes: "", created_at: "", alert_limit: 4.5, critical_limit: 7.1 };
    const rs = [
      { ...base, technique: "vibracao", measured_at: "2026-01-01", value: 9 },
      { ...base, technique: "vibracao", measured_at: "2026-02-01", value: 5 },
    ];
    expect(suggestedStatus(rs)).toBe("atencao");
  });
});

  });
});

import { filterRecords } from "./machines";

    { performed_at: "2026-09-10", machine_id: "a", kind: "preventiva" },
    { performed_at: "2026-10-10", machine_id: "a", kind: "corretiva" },
    { performed_at: "2026-10-20", machine_id: "b", kind: "preventiva" },
  ];

    expect(filterRecords(rs, { from: "2026-10-10", to: "2026-10-20" }).length).toBe(2);
  });

    expect(filterRecords(rs, { machineId: "a", kind: "preventiva" })).toEqual([rs[0]]);
  });
});

import { comparePeriods, checklistCsv } from "./machines";
describe("comparação e CSV", () => {
  const g = (c: number, t: number) => ({ machine_id: "m", kind: "preventiva", checked: c, total: t, items: [{ item: "Óleo", total: t, missed: t - c }] });
  it("compara taxas entre dois períodos", () => {
    expect(comparePeriods([g(1, 2)], [g(3, 4)])).toEqual([{ machine_id: "m", kind: "preventiva", a: 50, b: 75, diff: 25 }]);
  });
  it("período sem dados fica vazio", () => {
    expect(comparePeriods([], [g(1, 2)])[0]).toMatchObject({ a: null, b: 50, diff: null });
  });
  it("gera uma linha por item no CSV", () => {
    const csv = checklistCsv([g(1, 2)], new Map([["m", "Prensa"]]));
    expect(csv.split("\n")[1]).toBe('"Prensa";"preventiva";"50";"Óleo";"1";"2";"50";"sim"');
  });
});

describe("peças", () => {

  const ps = [p({}), p({ name: "Correia", supplier: "Gates", status: "comprar", machine_id: "b", created_at: "2026-10-20T12:00:00Z" })];
  it("busca por nome ou fornecedor sem acento", () => {
    expect(filterParts(ps, { term: "gates" })).toEqual([ps[1]]);
  });
  it("filtra por máquina, situação e data", () => {
    expect(filterParts(ps, { machineId: "a", status: "quebrada", from: "2026-10-10", to: "2026-10-10" })).toEqual([ps[0]]);
  });
  it("custo = quantidade × preço unitário", () => {
    expect(partsCost([{ quantity: 3, unit_price: 25.5 }, { quantity: 2, unit_price: null }])).toBe(76.5);
  });
});

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/lib/machines.ts

import { queryOptions } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import type { Tables } from "@/integrations/supabase/types";

export type Machine = Tables<"machines">;
export type MaintenanceRecord = Tables<"maintenance_records">;
export type Manual = Tables<"manuals">;
export type Spec = { label: string; value: string };

export const STATUS = {
  ok: { label: "Operando", tone: "success" },
  atencao: { label: "Atenção", tone: "warning" },
  critico: { label: "Crítico", tone: "destructive" },
} as const;
export type StatusKey = keyof typeof STATUS;

export const MAINTENANCE_KINDS = ["preventiva", "corretiva", "preditiva"] as const;

export const machinesQuery = () =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["machines"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("machines")
        .select("*")
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export const machineQuery = (id: string) =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["machine", id],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("machines").select("*").eq("id", id).maybeSingle();
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export const maintenanceQuery = (machineId: string) =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["maintenance", machineId],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("maintenance_records")
        .select("*")
        .eq("machine_id", machineId)
        .order("performed_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export const manualsQuery = (machineId: string) =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["manuals", machineId],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("manuals")
        .select("*")
        .eq("machine_id", machineId)
        .order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export function parseSpecs(specs: unknown): Spec[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(specs)) return [];
  return specs.filter(
    (s): s is Spec => !!s && typeof s === "object" && "label" in s && "value" in s,
  );
}

/** Case/accent-insensitive search across the main identifying fields. */
export function matchesSearch(m: Pick<Machine, "name" | "manufacturer" | "model" | "serial_number" | "location" | "category">, term: string) {
  const norm = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{Diacritic}/gu, "").toLowerCase();
  const t = norm(term.trim());
  if (!t) return true;
  return [m.name, m.manufacturer, m.model, m.serial_number, m.location, m.category].some((f) =>
    norm(f ?? "").includes(t),
  );
}

/** External search links for manufacturer manuals, built from manufacturer + model. */
export function manualSearchLinks(m: Pick<Machine, "manufacturer" | "model" | "name">) {
  const base = [m.manufacturer, m.model || m.name].filter(Boolean).join(" ").trim();
  const q = (s: string) => encodeURIComponent(s);
  return [
    { label: "Manual em PDF", href: `https://www.google.com/search?q=${q(`${base} manual filetype:pdf`)}` },
    { label: "Site do fabricante", href: `https://www.google.com/search?q=${q(`${base} manual site oficial`)}` },
    { label: "ManualsLib", href: `https://www.manualslib.com/brand/?q=${q(base)}` },
  ];
}

export type Inspection = Tables<"inspections">;

export const TECHNIQUES = {
  vibracao: { label: "Vibração", unit: "mm/s" },
  temperatura: { label: "Temperatura", unit: "°C" },
  ruido: { label: "Ruído", unit: "dB" },
  oleo: { label: "Análise de óleo", unit: "ppm" },
  termografia: { label: "Termografia", unit: "°C" },
} as const;
export type TechniqueKey = keyof typeof TECHNIQUES;

export const inspectionsQuery = (machineId: string) =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["inspections", machineId],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from("inspections")
        .select("*")
        .eq("machine_id", machineId)
        .order("measured_at", { ascending: true });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

/** Reading level: critical when value >= critical limit, alert when >= alert limit. */
export function readingLevel(r: { value: number; alert_limit: number | null; critical_limit: number | null }): StatusKey {
  if (r.critical_limit != null && r.value >= r.critical_limit) return "critico";
  if (r.alert_limit != null && r.value >= r.alert_limit) return "atencao";
  return "ok";
}

/** Machine level suggested by the latest reading of each technique (worst wins). */
export function suggestedStatus(readings: Inspection[]): StatusKey {
  const latest = new Map<string, Inspection>();
  for (const r of readings) {
    const prev = latest.get(r.technique);
    if (!prev || r.measured_at >= prev.measured_at) latest.set(r.technique, r);
  }
  const levels = [...latest.values()].map((r) =>
    readingLevel({ value: Number(r.value), alert_limit: r.alert_limit == null ? null : Number(r.alert_limit), critical_limit: r.critical_limit == null ? null : Number(r.critical_limit) }),
  );
  return levels.includes("critico") ? "critico" : levels.includes("atencao") ? "atencao" : "ok";
}

export const allInspectionsQuery = () =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["inspections", "all"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("inspections").select("*");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

/** Days from `today` until `date` (negative = overdue). Both as YYYY-MM-DD. */
export function daysUntil(date: string, today: string): number {
  return Math.round((Date.parse(date + "T00:00Z") - Date.parse(today + "T00:00Z")) / 86400000);
}

/** Maintenance is "upcoming" when overdue or due within `windowDays`. */
type Due = { next_maintenance_at: string | null; next_maintenance_done?: boolean | null };
export function upcomingMaintenance<T extends Due>(ms: T[], today: string, windowDays = 30) {
  return ms
    .filter((m) => !m.next_maintenance_done && m.next_maintenance_at && daysUntil(m.next_maintenance_at, today) <= windowDays)
    .sort((a, b) => a.next_maintenance_at!.localeCompare(b.next_maintenance_at!));
}

/** In-site notice window: maintenance due within this many days (or overdue). */
export const NOTICE_DAYS = 7;
export const dueSoon = <T extends Due>(ms: T[], today: string) =>
  upcomingMaintenance(ms, today, NOTICE_DAYS);

/** Total downtime hours across records (ignores empty values). */
export const totalDowntime = (rs: { downtime_hours: number | null }[]) =>
  rs.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.downtime_hours == null ? 0 : Number(r.downtime_hours)), 0);

/** Adds `days` to a YYYY-MM-DD date. */
export function addDays(date: string, days: number): string {
  return new Date(Date.parse(date + "T00:00Z") + days * 86400000).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

/** Machine changes after completing its maintenance: with an interval, schedule the next one from the completion date. */
export function afterCompletion(intervalDays: number | null, performedAt: string) {
  return intervalDays && intervalDays > 0
    ? { next_maintenance_at: addDays(performedAt, intervalDays), next_maintenance_done: false }
    : { next_maintenance_done: true };
}

export type ChecklistEntry = { item: string; checked: boolean };
export type MaintenancePhoto = { path: string; phase: "antes" | "depois" };

export const checklistTemplatesQuery = () =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["checklist-templates"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("checklist_templates").select("*");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

const strArr = (v: unknown): string[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter((x): x is string => typeof x === "string") : []);
export const templateItems = (ts: { kind: string; items: unknown }[], kind: string) =>
  strArr(ts.find((t) => t.kind === kind)?.items);

/** Records every template item with whether it was verified in this service. */
export const buildChecklist = (items: string[], checked: Set<string>): ChecklistEntry[] =>
  items.map((item) => ({ item, checked: checked.has(item) }));

export function parseChecklist(v: unknown): ChecklistEntry[] {
  return Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter((x): x is ChecklistEntry => !!x && typeof x === "object" && "item" in x) : [];
}
export function parsePhotos(v: unknown): MaintenancePhoto[] {
  return Array.isArray(v) ? v.filter((x): x is MaintenancePhoto => !!x && typeof x === "object" && "path" in x) : [];
}

/** Uploads photos under <user>/<machine>/<record>/ in the private bucket. */
export async function uploadMaintenancePhotos(machineId: string, recordId: string, files: { file: File; phase: MaintenancePhoto["phase"] }[]) {
  const { data: u } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  const out: MaintenancePhoto[] = [];
  for (const { file, phase } of files) {
    const path = `${u.user!.id}/${machineId}/${recordId}/${phase}-${crypto.randomUUID()}-${file.name.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, "_")}`;
    const { error } = await supabase.storage.from("maintenance-photos").upload(path, file);
    if (!error) out.push({ path, phase });
  }
  return out;
}

export const allMaintenanceQuery = () =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["maintenance", "all"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("maintenance_records").select("*");
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export type ItemStat = { item: string; total: number; missed: number };
export type ChecklistGroup = { machine_id: string; kind: string; checked: number; total: number; items: ItemStat[] };

/** Completion rate per machine + maintenance kind, with per-item miss counts (most missed first). */
export function checklistStats(records: { machine_id: string; kind: string; checklist: unknown }[]): ChecklistGroup[] {
  const groups = new Map<string, ChecklistGroup>();
  for (const r of records) {
    const cl = parseChecklist(r.checklist);
    if (!cl.length) continue;
    const key = r.machine_id + "|" + r.kind;
    const g = groups.get(key) ?? { machine_id: r.machine_id, kind: r.kind, checked: 0, total: 0, items: [] };
    for (const c of cl) {
      g.total++;
      if (c.checked) g.checked++;
      let s = g.items.find((i) => i.item === c.item);
      if (!s) g.items.push((s = { item: c.item, total: 0, missed: 0 }));
      s.total++;
      if (!c.checked) s.missed++;
    }
    groups.set(key, g);
  }
  for (const g of groups.values()) g.items.sort((a, b) => b.missed / b.total - a.missed / a.total);
  return [...groups.values()].sort((a, b) => a.checked / a.total - b.checked / b.total);
}

/** An item is "frequently missed" when skipped in at least half of the services (min. 2 occurrences). */
export const frequentlyMissed = (s: ItemStat) => s.total >= 2 && s.missed / s.total >= 0.5;

export type RecordFilter = { from?: string; to?: string; machineId?: string; kind?: string };
/** Keeps records inside the period (inclusive) and matching machine/kind when set. */
export const filterRecords = <T extends { performed_at: string; machine_id: string; kind: string }>(rs: T[], f: RecordFilter) =>
  rs.filter((r) =>
    (!f.from || r.performed_at >= f.from) && (!f.to || r.performed_at <= f.to) &&
    (!f.machineId || r.machine_id === f.machineId) && (!f.kind || r.kind === f.kind));

const rate = (g: { checked: number; total: number }) => (g.total ? Math.round((g.checked / g.total) * 100) : null);
export type PeriodRow = { machine_id: string; kind: string; a: number | null; b: number | null; diff: number | null };
/** Side-by-side completion rate per machine + kind for two period group sets (diff = B − A, points). */
export function comparePeriods(a: ChecklistGroup[], b: ChecklistGroup[]): PeriodRow[] {
  const keys = new Map<string, { machine_id: string; kind: string }>();
  [...a, ...b].forEach((g) => keys.set(g.machine_id + "|" + g.kind, g));
  return [...keys.entries()].map(([k, g]) => {
    const ra = a.find((x) => x.machine_id + "|" + x.kind === k), rb = b.find((x) => x.machine_id + "|" + x.kind === k);
    const va = ra ? rate(ra) : null, vb = rb ? rate(rb) : null;
    return { machine_id: g.machine_id, kind: g.kind, a: va, b: vb, diff: va != null && vb != null ? vb - va : null };
  });
}

/** CSV (semicolon, pt-BR Excel friendly) with one row per checklist item of each machine + kind. */
export function checklistCsv(groups: ChecklistGroup[], names: Map<string, string>): string {
  const esc = (v: string | number) => `"${String(v).replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  const rows = [["Máquina", "Tipo", "Taxa do grupo (%)", "Item", "Verificados", "Total", "Taxa do item (%)", "Frequentemente esquecido"]];
  for (const g of groups) for (const i of g.items)
    rows.push([names.get(g.machine_id) ?? "Máquina", g.kind, String(rate(g)), i.item, String(i.total - i.missed), String(i.total), String(Math.round(((i.total - i.missed) / i.total) * 100)), frequentlyMissed(i) ? "sim" : "não"]);
  return rows.map((r) => r.map(esc).join(";")).join("\n");
}

export type Part = Tables<"parts">;
export const PART_STATUS = {
  quebrada: { label: "Quebrada", cls: "text-destructive" },
  comprar: { label: "Precisa comprar", cls: "text-primary" },
  comprada: { label: "Comprada", cls: "text-success" },
} as const;
export type PartStatus = keyof typeof PART_STATUS;
export const partsQuery = () =>
  queryOptions({
    queryKey: ["parts"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from("parts").select("*").order("created_at", { ascending: false });
      if (error) throw error;
      return data;
    },
  });

export type PartFilter = { term?: string; machineId?: string; status?: string; from?: string; to?: string };
/** Filters parts by text (name/supplier/notes, accent-insensitive), machine, status and registration date (inclusive). */
export function filterParts<T extends { name: string; supplier: string; notes: string; machine_id: string | null; status: string; created_at: string }>(ps: T[], f: PartFilter) {
  const norm = (s: string) => s.normalize("NFD").replace(/\p{Diacritic}/gu, "").toLowerCase();
  const t = norm((f.term ?? "").trim());
  return ps.filter((p) => {
    const d = p.created_at.slice(0, 10);
    return (!t || [p.name, p.supplier, p.notes].some((x) => norm(x).includes(t))) &&
      (!f.machineId || p.machine_id === f.machineId) && (!f.status || p.status === f.status) &&
      (!f.from || d >= f.from) && (!f.to || d <= f.to);
  });
}
/** Total cost = quantity × unit price, ignoring parts without price. */
export const partsCost = (ps: { quantity: number; unit_price: number | null }[]) =>
  ps.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.unit_price == null ? 0 : p.quantity * Number(p.unit_price)), 0);

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/lib/maintenance-report.ts

import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { parseChecklist, parsePhotos, type Machine, type MaintenanceRecord } from "@/lib/machines";

async function toJpeg(url: string) {
  const img = new Image();
  img.crossOrigin = "anonymous";
  img.src = url;
  await img.decode();
  const max = 900, s = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight));
  const c = document.createElement("canvas");
  c.width = img.naturalWidth * s; c.height = img.naturalHeight * s;
  c.getContext("2d")!.drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  return { data: c.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.85), w: c.width, h: c.height };
}

/** Builds the PDF report of one completed maintenance and downloads it. */
export async function downloadMaintenanceReport(machine: Machine, r: MaintenanceRecord) {
  const { jsPDF } = await import("jspdf");
  const doc = new jsPDF({ unit: "mm", format: "a4" });
  const W = 210, M = 15;
  let y = M;
  const need = (h: number) => { if (y + h > 297 - M) { doc.addPage(); y = M; } };
  const text = (t: string, size = 10, bold = false) => {
    doc.setFont("helvetica", bold ? "bold" : "normal").setFontSize(size);
    const lines = doc.splitTextToSize(t, W - 2 * M) as string[];
    need(lines.length * size * 0.45);
    doc.text(lines, M, y + size * 0.35);
    y += lines.length * size * 0.45 + 1.5;
  };
  const section = (t: string) => { y += 3; text(t.toUpperCase(), 9, true); doc.setDrawColor(200).line(M, y - 1, W - M, y - 1); y += 1; };
  const date = new Date(r.performed_at + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");

  text("MaqSense — Relatório de manutenção", 16, true);
  text(`${machine.name}${machine.model ? " · " + machine.model : ""}${machine.serial_number ? " · Nº " + machine.serial_number : ""}`, 11);
  section("Dados do serviço");
  text(`Data: ${date}`);
  text(`Tipo: ${r.kind}`);
  text(`Responsável: ${r.technician || "Não informado"}`);
  if (r.downtime_hours != null) text(`Tempo parado: ${Number(r.downtime_hours).toLocaleString("pt-BR")} h`);
  if (r.cost != null) text(`Custo: R$ ${Number(r.cost).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}`);
  section("Observações");
  text(r.description || "Sem observações.");
  section("Peças utilizadas");
  text(r.parts_used || "Nenhuma peça informada.");

  const cl = parseChecklist(r.checklist);
  section(`Checklist (${cl.filter((c) => c.checked).length}/${cl.length})`);
  if (!cl.length) text("Sem checklist neste serviço.");
  cl.forEach((c) => text(`[${c.checked ? "X" : "  "}] ${c.item}${c.checked ? "" : "  (não verificado)"}`));

  const photos = parsePhotos(r.photos);
  if (photos.length) {
    const { data } = await supabase.storage.from("maintenance-photos").createSignedUrls(photos.map((p) => p.path), 600);
    for (const phase of ["antes", "depois"] as const) {
      const ps = photos.filter((p) => p.phase === phase);
      if (!ps.length) continue;
      section(`Fotos ${phase}`);
      let x = M; const boxW = (W - 2 * M - 5) / 2, boxH = 60;
      for (const p of ps) {
        const url = data?.find((d) => d.path === p.path)?.signedUrl;
        if (!url) continue;
        try {
          const img = await toJpeg(url);
          const s = Math.min(boxW / img.w, boxH / img.h);
          if (x === M) need(boxH + 3);
          doc.addImage(img.data, "JPEG", x, y, img.w * s, img.h * s);
          if (x === M) x = M + boxW + 5; else { x = M; y += boxH + 3; }
        } catch { /* skip unreadable image */ }
      }
      if (x !== M) y += boxH + 3;
    }
  }

  doc.setFontSize(8).setTextColor(130).text(`Gerado em ${new Date().toLocaleString("pt-BR")}`, M, 297 - 8);
  doc.save(`manutencao-${machine.name.replace(/[^\w-]+/g, "_")}-${r.performed_at}.pdf`);
}

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";

export type ManualSuggestion = { title: string; kind: string; why: string; query: string };

/** AI suggestions of which manufacturer documents to look for, based on the machine's data. */
export const suggestManuals = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .inputValidator((input: { machineId: string }) => input)
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const { data: m, error } = await context.supabase
      .from("machines")
      .select("name, manufacturer, model, category, specs")
      .eq("id", data.machineId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (error || !m) throw new Error("Máquina não encontrada.");

    const apiKey = process.env["LOVABLE_API_KEY"];
    if (!apiKey) throw new Error("IA não configurada.");

    const [{ createOpenAI }, { streamText, Output, NoObjectGeneratedError }, { z }] = await Promise.all([
      import("@ai-sdk/openai"),
      import("ai"),
      import("zod"),
    ]);

    let runId: string | undefined;
    const provider = createOpenAI({
      baseURL: "https://ai.gateway.lovable.dev/v1",
      apiKey,
      headers: { "Lovable-API-Key": apiKey, "X-Lovable-AIG-SDK": "vercel-ai-sdk" },
      fetch: async (input, init) => {
        const headers = new Headers(init?.headers);
        if (runId) headers.set("X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID", runId);
        const res = await fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
        runId ??= res.headers.get("X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID") ?? undefined;
        return res;
      },
    });

    const schema = z.object({
      suggestions: z.array(
        z.object({ title: z.string(), kind: z.string(), why: z.string(), query: z.string() }),
      ),
    });

    const result = streamText({
      model: provider.responses("openai/gpt-6-astra"),
      system:
        "Você é um especialista em manutenção industrial. Responda em português do Brasil. " +
        "Sugira de 3 a 6 documentos do fabricante úteis para manutenção preditiva do equipamento " +
        "(manual de operação, manual de manutenção, catálogo de peças, folha de dados, boletins). " +
        "Não invente URLs. Para cada um, dê: title (nome provável do documento), kind (tipo curto), " +
        "why (uma frase sobre por que é útil) e query (termos de busca no Google para encontrá-lo, em inglês ou português).",
      prompt: `Equipamento: ${m.name}\nFabricante: ${m.manufacturer || "?"}\nModelo: ${m.model || "?"}\nCategoria: ${m.category || "?"}\nEspecificações: ${JSON.stringify(m.specs)}`,
      output: Output.object({ schema }),
      providerOptions: {
        openai: {
          forceReasoning: true,
          reasoningEffort: "low",
          reasoningSummary: "auto",
          store: false,
          include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"],
        },
      },
    });

    try {
      const out = await result.output;
      return { suggestions: out.suggestions.slice(0, 6) as ManualSuggestion[] };
    } catch (e) {
      if (NoObjectGeneratedError.isInstance(e)) throw new Error("A IA não retornou sugestões válidas.");
      const status = (e as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
      if (status === 402) throw new Error("Créditos de IA esgotados.");
      if (status === 429) throw new Error("Muitas solicitações. Tente novamente em instantes.");
      throw new Error("Não foi possível gerar sugestões agora.");
    }
  });

import { createServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { requireSupabaseAuth } from "@/integrations/supabase/auth-middleware";

export type MaintenancePlan = {
  steps: { title: string; detail: string; minutes: number }[];
  parts: { name: string; qty: string }[];
  tools: string[];
  tips: string[];
};

/** AI checklist to make the next maintenance faster: steps, parts to set aside, tools, time-saving tips. */
export const planMaintenance = createServerFn({ method: "POST" })
  .middleware([requireSupabaseAuth])
  .inputValidator((input: { machineId: string }) => input)
  .handler(async ({ data, context }) => {
    const { data: m } = await context.supabase
      .from("machines")
      .select("name, manufacturer, model, category, specs, notes")
      .eq("id", data.machineId)
      .maybeSingle();
    if (!m) throw new Error("Máquina não encontrada.");
    const { data: hist } = await context.supabase
      .from("maintenance_records")
      .select("performed_at, kind, description, downtime_hours")
      .eq("machine_id", data.machineId)
      .order("performed_at", { ascending: false })
      .limit(10);
    const { data: insp } = await context.supabase
      .from("inspections")
      .select("measured_at, technique, value, unit, alert_limit, critical_limit")
      .eq("machine_id", data.machineId)
      .order("measured_at", { ascending: false })
      .limit(10);

    const apiKey = process.env["LOVABLE_API_KEY"];
    if (!apiKey) throw new Error("IA não configurada.");

    const [{ createOpenAI }, { streamText, Output, NoObjectGeneratedError }, { z }] = await Promise.all([
      import("@ai-sdk/openai"),
      import("ai"),
      import("zod"),
    ]);

    let runId: string | undefined;
    const provider = createOpenAI({
      baseURL: "https://ai.gateway.lovable.dev/v1",
      apiKey,
      headers: { "Lovable-API-Key": apiKey, "X-Lovable-AIG-SDK": "vercel-ai-sdk" },
      fetch: async (input, init) => {
        const headers = new Headers(init?.headers);
        if (runId) headers.set("X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID", runId);
        const res = await fetch(input, { ...init, headers });
        runId ??= res.headers.get("X-Lovable-AIG-Run-ID") ?? undefined;
        return res;
      },
    });

    const schema = z.object({
      steps: z.array(z.object({ title: z.string(), detail: z.string(), minutes: z.number() })),
      parts: z.array(z.object({ name: z.string(), qty: z.string() })),
      tools: z.array(z.string()),
      tips: z.array(z.string()),
    });

    const result = streamText({
      model: provider.responses("openai/gpt-6-astra"),
      system:
        "Você é um especialista em manutenção industrial. Responda em português do Brasil. " +
        "Monte um plano para a próxima manutenção preventiva do equipamento que reduza o tempo de máquina parada: " +
        "steps (6 a 12 passos em ordem, com detalhe curto e minutos estimados; inclua segurança/bloqueio de energia), " +
        "parts (peças e consumíveis a separar ANTES da parada, com quantidade provável), tools (ferramentas e instrumentos), " +
        "tips (3 a 5 dicas para ganhar tempo, ex.: o que preparar com a máquina ainda rodando). " +
        "Use o histórico e as inspeções para priorizar problemas recorrentes. Seja prático e não invente códigos de peça.",
      prompt:
        `Equipamento: ${m.name}\nFabricante: ${m.manufacturer || "?"}\nModelo: ${m.model || "?"}\nCategoria: ${m.category || "?"}\n` +
        `Especificações: ${JSON.stringify(m.specs)}\nObservações: ${m.notes || "-"}\n` +
        `Histórico recente: ${JSON.stringify(hist ?? [])}\nInspeções recentes: ${JSON.stringify(insp ?? [])}`,
      output: Output.object({ schema }),
      providerOptions: {
        openai: {
          forceReasoning: true,
          reasoningEffort: "low",
          reasoningSummary: "auto",
          store: false,
          include: ["reasoning.encrypted_content"],
        },
      },
    });

    try {
      return (await result.output) as MaintenancePlan;
    } catch (e) {
      if (NoObjectGeneratedError.isInstance(e)) throw new Error("A IA não retornou um plano válido.");
      const status = (e as { statusCode?: number }).statusCode;
      if (status === 402) throw new Error("Créditos de IA esgotados.");
      if (status === 429) throw new Error("Muitas solicitações. Tente novamente em instantes.");
      throw new Error("Não foi possível gerar o plano agora.");
    }
  });

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/README.md
####################################################################################################

# Routes

TanStack Start uses **file-based routing**. Every `.tsx` file in this directory
defines a route. Do **not** create `src/pages/`, `src/routes/_app/index.tsx`, or
`app/layout.tsx` — those are Next.js / Remix conventions. The only root layout
is `src/routes/__root.tsx`.

## Conventions

| File | URL |
| --- | --- |
| `index.tsx` | `/` |
| `about.tsx` | `/about` |
| `users/index.tsx` | `/users` |
| `users/$id.tsx` | `/users/:id` (dynamic — bare `$`, no curly braces) |
| `posts/{-$category}.tsx` | `/posts/:category?` (optional segment) |
| `files/$.tsx` | `/files/*` (splat — read via `_splat` param, never `*`) |
| `_layout.tsx` | layout route (renders children via `<Outlet />`) |
| `__root.tsx` | app shell — wraps every page; preserve `<Outlet />` |

`routeTree.gen.ts` is auto-generated. Don't edit it by hand.


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/__root.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import {
  Outlet,
  Link,
  createRootRouteWithContext,
  useRouter,
  HeadContent,
  Scripts,
  type ErrorComponentProps,
} from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";

import appCss from "../styles.css?url";
import { reportLovableError } from "../lib/lovable-error-reporting";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";

function NotFoundComponent() {
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-7xl font-bold text-foreground">404</h1>
        <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold text-foreground">Page not found</h2>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          The page you're looking for doesn't exist or has been moved.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6">
          <Link
            to="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90"
          >
            Go home
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function ErrorComponent({ error, reset }: ErrorComponentProps) {
  console.error(error);
  const router = useRouter();
  useEffect(() => {
    reportLovableError(error, { boundary: "tanstack_root_error_component" });
  }, [error]);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-background px-4">
      <div className="max-w-md text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-foreground">
          This page didn't load
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Something went wrong on our end. You can try refreshing or head back home.
        </p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              router.invalidate();
              reset();
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-primary/90"
          >
            Try again
          </button>
          <a
            href="/"
            className="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md border border-input bg-background px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-accent"
          >
            Go home
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const Route = createRootRouteWithContext<{ queryClient: QueryClient }>()({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { charSet: "utf-8" },
      { name: "viewport", content: "width=device-width, initial-scale=1" },
      { title: "MaqSense — Inspeções e manutenção preditiva" },
      { name: "description", content: "Cadastro de máquinas, especificações, histórico de manutenção e manuais." },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary_large_image" },
    ],
    links: [
      { rel: "preconnect", href: "https://fonts.googleapis.com" },
      { rel: "preconnect", href: "https://fonts.gstatic.com", crossOrigin: "anonymous" },
      {
        rel: "stylesheet",
        href: "https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Archivo:wght@400;500;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500&display=swap",
      },
      { rel: "stylesheet", href: appCss },
      { rel: "icon", href: "/favicon.png", type: "image/png" },
    ],
  }),
  shellComponent: RootShell,
  component: RootComponent,
  notFoundComponent: NotFoundComponent,
  errorComponent: ErrorComponent,
});

function RootShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <html lang="pt-BR">
      <head>
        <HeadContent />
      </head>
      <body>
        {children}
        <Scripts />
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

function RootComponent() {
  const { queryClient } = Route.useRouteContext();
  const router = useRouter();

  useEffect(() => {
    const { data } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event) => {
      if (event !== "SIGNED_IN" && event !== "SIGNED_OUT" && event !== "USER_UPDATED") return;
      router.invalidate();
      if (event !== "SIGNED_OUT") queryClient.invalidateQueries();
    });
    return () => data.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [router, queryClient]);

  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <Outlet />
      <Toaster />
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/auth.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import "@lovable.dev/cloud-auth-js/styles.css";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Logo } from "@/components/AppHeader";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { lovable } from "@/integrations/lovable/index";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/auth")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Entrar — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Acesse sua conta MaqSense para gerenciar máquinas e manutenções." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Entrar — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Acesse sua conta MaqSense." },
    ],
  }),
  component: AuthPage,
});

function AuthPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"in" | "up">("in");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    supabase.auth.getUser().then(({ data }) => {
      if (data.user) navigate({ to: "/maquinas", replace: true });
    });
    const { data } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((e, s) => {
      if (e === "SIGNED_IN" && s) navigate({ to: "/maquinas", replace: true });
    });
    return () => data.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, [navigate]);

  async function submit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    if (mode === "in") {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
      if (error) toast.error("E-mail ou senha incorretos.");
    } else {
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({
        email,
        password,
        options: { emailRedirectTo: window.location.origin },
      });
      if (error) toast.error(error.message);
      else toast.success("Conta criada! Confirme pelo link enviado ao seu e-mail.");
    }
    setLoading(false);
  }

  async function oauth(provider: "google" | "lovable") {
    const r = await lovable.auth.signInWithOAuth(provider, { redirect_uri: window.location.origin });
    if (r.error) toast.error("Não foi possível entrar.");
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid min-h-screen place-items-center bg-blueprint px-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-sm border bg-card p-8">
        <Logo />
        <h1 className="mt-6 text-2xl font-bold">{mode === "in" ? "Entrar" : "Criar conta"}</h1>
        <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-6 space-y-4">
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="email">E-mail</Label>
            <Input id="email" type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <Label htmlFor="pw">Senha</Label>
            <Input id="pw" type="password" required minLength={6} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={loading}>
            {mode === "in" ? "Entrar" : "Criar conta"}
          </Button>
        </form>
        <div className="my-5 flex items-center gap-3 label-tech">
          <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-border" />ou<span className="h-px flex-1 bg-border" />
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <Button variant="outline" className="w-full" onClick={() => oauth("google")}>
            Continuar com Google
          </Button>
          <button type="button" className="lovable-auth-button w-full" onClick={() => oauth("lovable")}>
            Continue with Lovable
          </button>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          className="mt-6 w-full text-center text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          onClick={() => setMode(mode === "in" ? "up" : "in")}
        >
          {mode === "in" ? "Não tem conta? Criar conta" : "Já tem conta? Entrar"}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { ClipboardList, FileText, Search, Settings2 } from "lucide-react";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "MaqSense — Inspeções e manutenção preditiva" },
      { name: "description", content: "Cadastre máquinas, consulte especificações, registre manutenções e encontre manuais dos fabricantes." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "MaqSense — Inspeções e manutenção preditiva" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Cadastro e pesquisa de máquinas, histórico de manutenção e manuais." },

const features = [
  { icon: Search, title: "Pesquisa de máquinas", text: "Busque por nome, fabricante, modelo, série ou local." },
