####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/checklists.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, FileDown, FileSpreadsheet } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { downloadChecklistReport } from "@/lib/checklist-report";
import { MAINTENANCE_KINDS, filterRecords, allMaintenanceQuery, checklistStats, frequentlyMissed, machinesQuery, comparePeriods, checklistCsv } from "@/lib/machines";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/checklists")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Checklists — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Taxa de conclusão dos checklists por máquina e tipo de manutenção." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Checklists — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Itens de checklist mais esquecidos em cada máquina." },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary" },
    ],
  }),
  loader: ({ context }) =>
    Promise.all([
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(machinesQuery()),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(allMaintenanceQuery()),
    ]),
  component: ChecklistsDashboard,
});

const pct = (a: number, b: number) => Math.round((a / b) * 100);

function ChecklistsDashboard() {
  const { data: machines } = useSuspenseQuery(machinesQuery());
  const { data: records } = useSuspenseQuery(allMaintenanceQuery());
  const [f, setF] = useState({ from: "", to: "", machineId: "", kind: "" });
  const groups = checklistStats(filterRecords(records, f));
  const names = new Map(machines.map((m) => [m.id, m.name]));
  const br = (d: string) => new Date(d + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");
  const filterLabel = [
    f.from || f.to ? `período ${f.from ? br(f.from) : "início"} a ${f.to ? br(f.to) : "hoje"}` : "todo o período",
    f.machineId ? names.get(f.machineId) : "todas as máquinas",
    f.kind || "todos os tipos",
  ].join(" · ");
  const sel = "h-9 rounded-sm border bg-background px-2 text-sm";
  const checked = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.checked, 0);
  const total = groups.reduce((s, g) => s + g.total, 0);
  const missed = groups.flatMap((g) => g.items.filter(frequentlyMissed).map((i) => ({ ...i, g })));
  const [cmp, setCmp] = useState({ aFrom: "", aTo: "", bFrom: "", bTo: "" });
  const base = { machineId: f.machineId, kind: f.kind };
  const cmpOn = !!(cmp.aFrom || cmp.aTo) && !!(cmp.bFrom || cmp.bTo);
  const rows = cmpOn ? comparePeriods(
    checklistStats(filterRecords(records, { ...base, from: cmp.aFrom, to: cmp.aTo })),
    checklistStats(filterRecords(records, { ...base, from: cmp.bFrom, to: cmp.bTo }))) : [];
  const exportCsv = () => {
    const blob = new Blob(["\uFEFF" + checklistCsv(groups, names)], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url; a.download = `checklists-${new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-CA")}.csv`;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove();
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 30000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <p className="label-tech">Checklists</p>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Taxa de conclusão</h1>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">De<Input type="date" value={f.from} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, from: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Até<Input type="date" value={f.to} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, to: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Máquina
          <select className={sel} value={f.machineId} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, machineId: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Todas</option>
            {machines.map((m) => <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.name}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Tipo
          <select className={sel} value={f.kind} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, kind: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Todos</option>
            {MAINTENANCE_KINDS.map((k) => <option key={k} value={k} className="capitalize">{k}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setF({ from: "", to: "", machineId: "", kind: "" })}>Limpar</Button>
        <Button size="sm" className="ml-auto" disabled={total === 0}
          onClick={() => downloadChecklistReport(groups, names, filterLabel).catch(() => toast.error("Não foi possível gerar o PDF."))}>
          <FileDown /> Relatório em PDF
        </Button>
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled={total === 0} onClick={exportCsv}>
          <FileSpreadsheet /> Exportar CSV
        </Button>
      </div>
      <section className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <p className="label-tech">Comparar dois períodos</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Usa a máquina e o tipo escolhidos acima.</p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
          <span className="text-sm font-semibold">Período A</span>
          <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">De<Input type="date" value={cmp.aFrom} onChange={(e) => setCmp({ ...cmp, aFrom: e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Até<Input type="date" value={cmp.aTo} onChange={(e) => setCmp({ ...cmp, aTo: e.target.value })} /></label>
          <span className="ml-4 text-sm font-semibold">Período B</span>
          <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">De<Input type="date" value={cmp.bFrom} onChange={(e) => setCmp({ ...cmp, bFrom: e.target.value })} /></label>
          <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Até<Input type="date" value={cmp.bTo} onChange={(e) => setCmp({ ...cmp, bTo: e.target.value })} /></label>
        </div>
        {!cmpOn ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Escolha as datas dos dois períodos para comparar.</p>
          : rows.length === 0 ? <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum checklist nesses períodos.</p> : (
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted-foreground"><tr><th className="py-1">Máquina</th><th>Tipo</th><th>Período A</th><th>Período B</th><th>Diferença</th></tr></thead>
            <tbody>{rows.map((r) => (
              <tr key={r.machine_id + r.kind} className="border-t">
                <td className="py-1">{names.get(r.machine_id) ?? "Máquina"}</td><td className="capitalize">{r.kind}</td>
                <td className="font-mono-ui">{r.a == null ? "—" : r.a + "%"}</td><td className="font-mono-ui">{r.b == null ? "—" : r.b + "%"}</td>
                <td className={`font-mono-ui ${r.diff == null ? "" : r.diff > 0 ? "text-success" : r.diff < 0 ? "text-destructive" : ""}`}>{r.diff == null ? "—" : (r.diff > 0 ? "+" : "") + r.diff + " pts"}</td>
              </tr>))}</tbody>
          </table>)}
      </section>
      {total === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          Nenhuma manutenção com checklist nesses filtros. Ajuste os filtros, ou crie um checklist na aba Histórico de uma máquina e conclua uma manutenção.
        </p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
            <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4"><p className="font-mono-ui text-3xl font-bold">{pct(checked, total)}%</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Itens verificados (geral)</p></div>
            <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4"><p className="font-mono-ui text-3xl font-bold">{groups.length}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Combinações máquina × tipo</p></div>
            <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4"><p className="font-mono-ui text-3xl font-bold text-destructive">{missed.length}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Itens frequentemente esquecidos</p></div>
          </div>

          {missed.length > 0 && (
            <section className="rounded-sm border border-destructive/50 bg-card p-4">
              <p className="label-tech mb-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-destructive"><AlertTriangle className="size-4" /> Frequentemente não verificados</p>
              <ul className="space-y-1 text-sm">
                {missed.map((i, k) => (
                  <li key={k}><b>{i.item}</b> — {names.get(i.g.machine_id) ?? "Máquina"} · <span className="capitalize">{i.g.kind}</span> · esquecido em {i.missed} de {i.total} serviços</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          )}

          <section className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            {groups.map((g) => {
              const r = pct(g.checked, g.total);
              return (
                <div key={g.machine_id + g.kind} className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
                  <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2">
                    <Link to="/maquinas/$id" params={{ id: g.machine_id }} className="font-semibold hover:text-primary">{names.get(g.machine_id) ?? "Máquina"}</Link>
                    <span className="font-mono-ui text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground">{g.kind}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div className="h-2 flex-1 rounded-full bg-secondary"><div className={`h-2 rounded-full ${r < 50 ? "bg-destructive" : r < 80 ? "bg-primary" : "bg-success"}`} style={{ width: `${r}%` }} /></div>
                    <span className="font-mono-ui text-sm">{r}%</span>
                  </div>
                  <ul className="space-y-1 text-xs">
                    {g.items.map((i) => (
                      <li key={i.item} className={`flex justify-between ${frequentlyMissed(i) ? "font-semibold text-destructive" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                        <span>{i.item}</span><span className="font-mono-ui">{i.total - i.missed}/{i.total}</span>
                      </li>
                    ))}
                  </ul>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/maquinas.$id.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link, notFound, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQueryClient, useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { ArrowLeft, ExternalLink, FileText, Pencil, Trash2, Upload } from "lucide-react";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { MachineForm } from "@/components/MachineForm";
import { InspectionsTab } from "@/components/InspectionsTab";
import { CompleteMaintenanceDialog } from "@/components/CompleteMaintenanceDialog";
import { ChecklistTemplatesEditor } from "@/components/ChecklistTemplatesEditor";
import { MaintenancePhotos } from "@/components/MaintenancePhotos";
import { downloadMaintenanceReport } from "@/lib/maintenance-report";
import { parseChecklist, parsePhotos, checklistTemplatesQuery, uploadMaintenancePhotos } from "@/lib/machines";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { Sparkles } from "lucide-react";
import { suggestManuals, type ManualSuggestion } from "@/lib/manual-ai.functions";

function AiSuggestions({ machineId }: { machineId: string }) {
  const run = useServerFn(suggestManuals);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<ManualSuggestion[] | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  async function go() {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      const r = await run({ data: { machineId } });
      setItems(r.suggestions);
    } catch (e) {
      toast.error(e instanceof Error ? e.message : "Falha ao gerar sugestões.");
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  return (
    <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <p className="label-tech">Sugestões da IA</p>
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={go} disabled={busy}>
          <Sparkles /> {busy ? "Pensando…" : items ? "Gerar de novo" : "Sugerir manuais"}
        </Button>
      </div>
      {!items && !busy && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">A IA indica quais documentos do fabricante procurar para esta máquina.</p>}
      {items?.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma sugestão.</p>}
      <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">
        {items?.map((s, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="rounded-sm border p-3">
            <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{s.title}</p>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-muted-foreground"><span className="mr-1 font-mono-ui uppercase text-primary">{s.kind}</span>{s.why}</p>
              </div>
              <Button asChild size="sm" variant="secondary">
                <a href={`https://www.google.com/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(s.query)}`} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Buscar <ExternalLink /></a>
              </Button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
import {
  MAINTENANCE_KINDS,
  inspectionsQuery,
  machineQuery,
  maintenanceQuery,
  manualSearchLinks,
  manualsQuery,
  parseSpecs,
  totalDowntime,
  type Machine,
} from "@/lib/machines";
import { MaintenancePlanCard } from "@/components/MaintenancePlanCard";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/maquinas/$id")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Detalhes da máquina — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Especificações, histórico de manutenção e manuais do equipamento." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Detalhes da máquina — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Ficha técnica do equipamento." },
    ],
  }),
  loader: async ({ context, params }) => {
    const m = await context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(machineQuery(params.id));
    if (!m) throw notFound();
    await Promise.all([
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(maintenanceQuery(params.id)),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(checklistTemplatesQuery()),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(manualsQuery(params.id)),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(inspectionsQuery(params.id)),
    ]);
  },
  notFoundComponent: () => (
    <div className="text-center">
      <p className="text-lg font-semibold">Máquina não encontrada.</p>
      <Link to="/maquinas" className="text-primary">Voltar à lista</Link>
    </div>
  ),
  component: MachineDetail,
});

function MachineDetail() {
  const { id } = Route.useParams();
  const { data: m } = useSuspenseQuery(machineQuery(id));
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(false);
  if (!m) return null;

  async function remove() {
    if (!confirm("Excluir esta máquina e todo o seu histórico?")) return;
    const { error } = await supabase.from("machines").delete().eq("id", id);
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível excluir.");
    await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machines"] });
    navigate({ to: "/maquinas" });
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <Link to="/maquinas" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground">
        <ArrowLeft className="size-4" /> Máquinas
      </Link>
      <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <p className="label-tech">{m.category || "Equipamento"} · {m.serial_number || "s/ série"}</p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">{m.name}</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-muted-foreground">
            {[m.manufacturer, m.model].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") || "Fabricante não informado"}
            {m.location && ` — ${m.location}`}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <StatusBadge status={m.status} />
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => setEditing((e) => !e)}><Pencil /> {editing ? "Cancelar" : "Editar"}</Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Excluir" onClick={remove}><Trash2 /></Button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {editing ? (
        <div className="mt-6 max-w-3xl">
          <MachineForm
            initial={m}
            submitLabel="Salvar alterações"
            onSubmit={async (v) => {
              const { error } = await supabase.from("machines").update({ ...v, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq("id", id);
              if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível salvar.");
              await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machine", id] });
              await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machines"] });
              setEditing(false);
              toast.success("Alterações salvas.");
            }}
          />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <Tabs defaultValue="manuais" className="mt-6">
          <TabsList>
            <TabsTrigger value="manuais">Manuais</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="specs">Especificações</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="historico">Histórico</TabsTrigger>
            <TabsTrigger value="inspecoes">Inspeções</TabsTrigger>
          </TabsList>
          <TabsContent value="manuais"><ManualsTab machine={m} /></TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="specs"><SpecsTab machine={m} /></TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="historico"><HistoryTab machineId={id} /></TabsContent>
          <TabsContent value="inspecoes"><InspectionsTab machine={m} /></TabsContent>
        </Tabs>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SpecsTab({ machine }: { machine: Machine }) {
  const specs = parseSpecs(machine.specs);
  const base = [
    { label: "Fabricante", value: machine.manufacturer },
    { label: "Modelo", value: machine.model },
    { label: "Nº de série", value: machine.serial_number },
    { label: "Instalação", value: machine.installed_at ? new Date(machine.installed_at + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR") : "" },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
      {[{ t: "Dados gerais", rows: base }, { t: "Parâmetros técnicos", rows: specs }].map((g) => (
        <div key={g.t} className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
          <p className="border-b p-4 label-tech">{g.t}</p>
          {g.rows.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum parâmetro cadastrado.</p>}
          {g.rows.map((r, i) => (
            <div key={i} className="flex justify-between border-b p-4 text-sm last:border-b-0">
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{r.label}</span>
              <span className="font-mono-ui">{r.value || "—"}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      ))}
      {machine.notes && <p className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4 text-sm md:col-span-2">{machine.notes}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

function HistoryTab({ machineId }: { machineId: string }) {
  const { data: machine } = useSuspenseQuery(machineQuery(machineId));
  const { data: records } = useSuspenseQuery(maintenanceQuery(machineId));
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ performed_at: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), kind: "preventiva", technician: "", description: "", parts_used: "", cost: "", downtime: "" });
  const [before, setBefore] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [after, setAfter] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const downtime = totalDowntime(records);
  const withDowntime = records.filter((r) => r.downtime_hours != null).length;

  async function add(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const { downtime: dt, ...rest } = form;
    const { data: rec, error } = await supabase.from("maintenance_records").insert({
      machine_id: machineId, ...rest, cost: form.cost ? Number(form.cost) : null,
      downtime_hours: dt ? Number(dt) : null,
    }).select("id").single();
    if (rec && (before.length || after.length)) {
      const photos = await uploadMaintenancePhotos(machineId, rec.id, [
        ...before.map((file) => ({ file, phase: "antes" as const })),
        ...after.map((file) => ({ file, phase: "depois" as const })),
      ]);
      await supabase.from("maintenance_records").update({ photos }).eq("id", rec.id);
      setBefore([]); setAfter([]); (e.target as HTMLFormElement).reset();
    }
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível registrar.");
    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, technician: "", description: "", parts_used: "", cost: "", downtime: "" }));
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["maintenance", machineId] });
    toast.success("Manutenção registrada.");
  }

  async function del(rid: string) {
    await supabase.from("maintenance_records").delete().eq("id", rid);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["maintenance", machineId] });
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
      <div className="space-y-6">
        {machine && machine.next_maintenance_at && !machine.next_maintenance_done && (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 rounded-sm border border-primary/40 bg-card p-4">
            <div>
              <p className="label-tech">Próxima manutenção · {new Date(machine.next_maintenance_at + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}</p>
              <p className="text-sm">{machine.next_maintenance_task || "Manutenção não descrita"}
                {machine.maintenance_interval_days ? <span className="text-muted-foreground"> · a cada {machine.maintenance_interval_days} dias</span> : null}</p>
            </div>
            <CompleteMaintenanceDialog machine={machine}><Button size="sm">Concluir</Button></CompleteMaintenanceDialog>
          </div>
        )}
        {withDowntime > 0 && (
          <div className="flex gap-6 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
            <div><p className="font-mono-ui text-2xl font-bold">{downtime.toLocaleString("pt-BR")} h</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Tempo parado total</p></div>
            <div><p className="font-mono-ui text-2xl font-bold">{(downtime / withDowntime).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })} h</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Média por manutenção</p></div>
          </div>
        )}
      <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
        {records.length === 0 && <p className="p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma manutenção registrada.</p>}
        {records.map((r) => (
          <div key={r.id} className="flex gap-4 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0">
            <div className="w-24 shrink-0 font-mono-ui text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              {new Date(r.performed_at + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <p className="text-sm"><span className="mr-2 rounded-sm bg-secondary px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono-ui text-xs uppercase">{r.kind}</span>{r.description || "Sem descrição"}</p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                {r.technician || "Técnico não informado"}
                {r.cost != null && ` · R$ ${Number(r.cost).toLocaleString("pt-BR", { minimumFractionDigits: 2 })}`}
                {r.downtime_hours != null && ` · ${Number(r.downtime_hours).toLocaleString("pt-BR")} h parada`}
              </p>
              {r.parts_used && <p className="mt-1 text-xs"><span className="label-tech mr-1">Peças:</span>{r.parts_used}</p>}
              {parseChecklist(r.checklist).length > 0 && (
                <ul className="mt-1 text-xs">
                  {parseChecklist(r.checklist).map((c, i) => (
                    <li key={i} className={c.checked ? "" : "text-muted-foreground line-through"}>{c.checked ? "✓" : "✗"} {c.item}</li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
              <MaintenancePhotos photos={parsePhotos(r.photos)} />
            </div>
            {machine && (
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Baixar relatório em PDF" title="Relatório em PDF"
                onClick={() => downloadMaintenanceReport(machine, r).catch(() => toast.error("Não foi possível gerar o PDF."))}><FileText /></Button>
            )}
            <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Excluir registro" onClick={() => del(r.id)}><Trash2 /></Button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
        <MaintenancePlanCard machineId={machineId} />
        <ChecklistTemplatesEditor />
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={add} className="h-fit space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <p className="label-tech">Registrar manutenção</p>
        <Input type="date" required value={form.performed_at} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, performed_at: e.target.value })} />
        <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
          {MAINTENANCE_KINDS.map((k) => (
            <button type="button" key={k} onClick={() => setForm({ ...form, kind: k })}
              className={`flex-1 rounded-sm px-2 py-1 text-xs capitalize ${form.kind === k ? "bg-secondary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>{k}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <Input placeholder="Técnico" value={form.technician} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, technician: e.target.value })} />
        <Textarea placeholder="O que foi feito?" value={form.description} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, description: e.target.value })} />
        <Input placeholder="Peças utilizadas" value={form.parts_used} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, parts_used: e.target.value })} />
        <Input type="number" step="0.01" placeholder="Custo (R$)" value={form.cost} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, cost: e.target.value })} />
        <Input type="number" step="0.1" min="0" placeholder="Tempo parado (horas)" value={form.downtime} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, downtime: e.target.value })} />
        <label className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">Fotos antes
          <Input type="file" accept="image/*" multiple onChange={(e) => setBefore([...(e.target.files ?? [])])} /></label>
        <label className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">Fotos depois
          <Input type="file" accept="image/*" multiple onChange={(e) => setAfter([...(e.target.files ?? [])])} /></label>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full">Registrar</Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

function ManualsTab({ machine }: { machine: Machine }) {
  const { data: manuals } = useSuspenseQuery(manualsQuery(machine.id));
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [url, setUrl] = useState("");
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const links = manualSearchLinks(machine);

  async function add(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!file && !url) return void toast.error("Envie um PDF ou informe um link.");
    setBusy(true);
    let file_path: string | null = null;
    if (file) {
      const { data: u } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
      file_path = `${u.user!.id}/${machine.id}/${Date.now()}-${file.name.replace(/[^\w.-]/g, "_")}`;
      const { error } = await supabase.storage.from("manuals").upload(file_path, file);
      if (error) { setBusy(false); return void toast.error("Falha no envio do arquivo."); }
    }
    const { error } = await supabase.from("manuals").insert({
      machine_id: machine.id, title: title || file?.name || "Manual", url: url || null, file_path,
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível salvar o manual.");
    setTitle(""); setUrl(""); setFile(null);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["manuals", machine.id] });
    toast.success("Manual adicionado.");
  }

  async function open(path: string) {
    const { data, error } = await supabase.storage.from("manuals").createSignedUrl(path, 300);
    if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível abrir.");
    window.open(data.signedUrl, "_blank", "noopener");
  }

  async function del(mid: string, path: string | null) {
    if (path) await supabase.storage.from("manuals").remove([path]);
    await supabase.from("manuals").delete().eq("id", mid);
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["manuals", machine.id] });
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
          <p className="border-b p-4 label-tech">Manuais salvos</p>
          {manuals.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhum manual salvo para esta máquina.</p>}
          {manuals.map((d) => (
            <div key={d.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0">
              <FileText className="size-4 text-primary" />
              <span className="flex-1 text-sm">{d.title}</span>
              {d.file_path && <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => open(d.file_path!)}>Abrir PDF</Button>}
              {d.url && <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm"><a href={d.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Link <ExternalLink /></a></Button>}
              <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Remover manual" onClick={() => del(d.id, d.file_path)}><Trash2 /></Button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
          <p className="label-tech">Buscar manual do fabricante</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Pesquisa para <span className="text-foreground">{[machine.manufacturer, machine.model || machine.name].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}</span>
          </p>
          <div className="mt-3 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {links.map((l) => (
              <Button key={l.label} asChild variant="secondary" size="sm">
                <a href={l.href} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">{l.label} <ExternalLink /></a>
              </Button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <AiSuggestions machineId={machine.id} />
      </div>
      <form onSubmit={add} className="space-y-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <p className="label-tech">Adicionar manual</p>
        <Input placeholder="Título" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="pdf" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><Upload className="size-4" /> Arquivo PDF</Label>
          <Input id="pdf" type="file" accept="application/pdf" onChange={(e) => setFile(e.target.files?.[0] ?? null)} />
        </div>
        <Input type="url" placeholder="ou link (https://…)" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} />
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={busy}>Salvar manual</Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/maquinas.index.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Plus, Search } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import { machinesQuery, matchesSearch, STATUS } from "@/lib/machines";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/maquinas/")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Máquinas — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Pesquise e filtre suas máquinas cadastradas." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Máquinas — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Pesquisa de máquinas cadastradas." },
    ],
  }),
  loader: ({ context }) => context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(machinesQuery()),
  component: MachinesPage,
});

function MachinesPage() {
  const { data: machines } = useSuspenseQuery(machinesQuery());
  const [term, setTerm] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<string>("todos");

  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => machines.filter((m) => matchesSearch(m, term) && (status === "todos" || m.status === status)),
    [machines, term, status],
  );

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <p className="label-tech">// Inventário</p>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Máquinas</h1>
        </div>
        <Button asChild>
          <Link to="/maquinas/nova"><Plus /> Cadastrar máquina</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <div className="relative min-w-64 flex-1">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 size-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />
          <Input
            className="pl-9"
            placeholder="Pesquisar por nome, fabricante, modelo, série ou local…"
            value={term}
            onChange={(e) => setTerm(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-sm border p-1">
          {["todos", ...Object.keys(STATUS)].map((s) => (
            <button
              key={s}
              onClick={() => setStatus(s)}
              className={`rounded-sm px-3 py-1 text-sm ${status === s ? "bg-secondary text-foreground" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}
            >
              {s === "todos" ? "Todos" : STATUS[s as keyof typeof STATUS].label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      <p className="mt-4 label-tech">{filtered.length} resultado(s)</p>

      {machines.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-4 rounded-sm border border-dashed bg-card p-12 text-center">
          <p className="font-semibold">Nenhuma máquina cadastrada ainda.</p>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Comece cadastrando o primeiro equipamento.</p>
          <Button asChild className="mt-4"><Link to="/maquinas/nova">Cadastrar máquina</Link></Button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-3 overflow-hidden rounded-sm border bg-card">
          {filtered.map((m) => (
            <Link
              key={m.id}
              to="/maquinas/$id"
              params={{ id: m.id }}
              className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_auto] items-center gap-4 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0 hover:bg-accent md:grid-cols-[2fr_1.5fr_1fr_auto]"
            >
              <div>
                <p className="font-semibold">{m.name}</p>
                <p className="font-mono-ui text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.serial_number || "sem nº de série"}</p>
              </div>
              <p className="hidden text-sm md:block">{[m.manufacturer, m.model].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") || "—"}</p>
              <p className="hidden text-sm text-muted-foreground md:block">{m.location || "—"}</p>
              <StatusBadge status={m.status} />
            </Link>
          ))}
          {filtered.length === 0 && <p className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">Nada encontrado para essa pesquisa.</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/maquinas.nova.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { MachineForm } from "@/components/MachineForm";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/maquinas/nova")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Cadastrar máquina — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Cadastre uma nova máquina com suas especificações." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Cadastrar máquina — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Cadastro de máquinas." },
    ],
  }),
  component: NewMachine,
});

function NewMachine() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <p className="label-tech">// Novo equipamento</p>
      <h1 className="mb-6 text-3xl font-bold">Cadastrar máquina</h1>
      <MachineForm
        submitLabel="Salvar máquina"
        onSubmit={async (v) => {
          const { data, error } = await supabase.from("machines").insert(v).select("id").single();
          if (error) return void toast.error("Não foi possível salvar.");
          await qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["machines"] });
          toast.success("Máquina cadastrada.");
          navigate({ to: "/maquinas/$id", params: { id: data.id } });
        }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/painel.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { AlertTriangle, CalendarClock, Cpu } from "lucide-react";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/StatusBadge";
import {
  allInspectionsQuery,
  daysUntil,
  machinesQuery,
  suggestedStatus,
  upcomingMaintenance,
  type StatusKey,
} from "@/lib/machines";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/painel")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Painel — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Máquinas em alerta e manutenções próximas." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Painel — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Visão geral das máquinas em alerta e das manutenções próximas." },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary" },
    ],
  }),
  loader: ({ context }) =>
    Promise.all([
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(machinesQuery()),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(allInspectionsQuery()),
    ]),
  component: Dashboard,
});

const rank: Record<StatusKey, number> = { ok: 0, atencao: 1, critico: 2 };

function Dashboard() {
  const { data: machines } = useSuspenseQuery(machinesQuery());
  const { data: inspections } = useSuspenseQuery(allInspectionsQuery());
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-CA");

  const alerts = machines
    .map((m) => {
      const fromReadings = suggestedStatus(inspections.filter((i) => i.machine_id === m.id));
      const set = (m.status in rank ? m.status : "ok") as StatusKey;
      const level = rank[fromReadings] > rank[set] ? fromReadings : set;
      return { m, level, byReading: rank[fromReadings] > 0 };
    })
    .filter((x) => x.level !== "ok")
    .sort((a, b) => rank[b.level] - rank[a.level]);

  const upcoming = upcomingMaintenance(machines, today);

  const stats = [
    { label: "Máquinas", value: machines.length, icon: Cpu },
    { label: "Em alerta", value: alerts.length, icon: AlertTriangle },
    { label: "Manutenções em 30 dias", value: upcoming.length, icon: CalendarClock },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <p className="label-tech">Visão geral</p>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Painel</h1>
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        {stats.map((s) => (
          <div key={s.label} className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-5">
            <s.icon className="size-4 text-primary" />
            <p className="mt-3 font-mono-ui text-3xl font-bold">{s.value}</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{s.label}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
          <p className="border-b p-4 label-tech">Máquinas em alerta</p>
          {alerts.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nenhuma máquina em alerta.</p>}
          {alerts.map(({ m, level, byReading }) => (
            <Link key={m.id} to="/maquinas/$id" params={{ id: m.id }} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0 hover:bg-secondary/50">
              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium">{m.name}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{m.location || "Local não informado"}{byReading && " · pela última inspeção"}</p>
              </div>
              <StatusBadge status={level} />
            </Link>
          ))}
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-sm border bg-card">
          <p className="border-b p-4 label-tech">Manutenções próximas</p>
          {upcoming.length === 0 && (
            <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Nada nos próximos 30 dias. Defina a "Próxima manutenção" ao editar uma máquina.</p>
          )}
          {upcoming.map((m) => {
            const d = daysUntil(m.next_maintenance_at!, today);
            return (
              <Link key={m.id} to="/maquinas/$id" params={{ id: m.id }} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b p-4 last:border-b-0 hover:bg-secondary/50">
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm font-medium">{m.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{new Date(m.next_maintenance_at! + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR")}</p>
                </div>
                <span className={`font-mono-ui text-xs ${d < 0 ? "text-destructive" : d <= 7 ? "text-primary" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}>
                  {d < 0 ? `${-d} dia(s) atrasada` : d === 0 ? "hoje" : `em ${d} dia(s)`}
                </span>
              </Link>
            );
          })}
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/pecas.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useQueryClient, useSuspenseQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useState } from "react";
import { Pencil, Trash2, Wrench } from "lucide-react";
import { toast } from "sonner";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import {
  PART_STATUS, type Part, type PartStatus, allMaintenanceQuery, filterParts, machinesQuery, partsCost, partsQuery,
} from "@/lib/machines";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated/pecas")({
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: "Peças — MaqSense" },
      { name: "description", content: "Registro de peças quebradas e das peças que precisam ser compradas." },
      { property: "og:title", content: "Peças — MaqSense" },
      { property: "og:description", content: "Controle de peças quebradas, compras e custos." },
      { property: "og:type", content: "website" },
      { name: "twitter:card", content: "summary" },
    ],
  }),
  loader: ({ context }) =>
    Promise.all([
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(machinesQuery()),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(partsQuery()),
      context.queryClient.ensureQueryData(allMaintenanceQuery()),
    ]),
  component: PartsPage,
});

const empty = {
  name: "", quantity: "1", machine_id: "", status: "quebrada" as PartStatus, notes: "",
  supplier: "", unit_price: "", purchased_at: "", maintenance_id: "",
};
const STATUSES = Object.keys(PART_STATUS) as PartStatus[];
const brl = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString("pt-BR", { style: "currency", currency: "BRL" });
const br = (d: string) => new Date(d.slice(0, 10) + "T00:00").toLocaleDateString("pt-BR");

function PartsPage() {
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const { data: machines } = useSuspenseQuery(machinesQuery());
  const { data: parts } = useSuspenseQuery(partsQuery());
  const { data: records } = useSuspenseQuery(allMaintenanceQuery());
  const [form, setForm] = useState(empty);
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [f, setF] = useState({ term: "", machineId: "", status: "", from: "", to: "" });
  const names = new Map(machines.map((m) => [m.id, m.name]));
  const recById = new Map(records.map((r) => [r.id, r]));
  const sel = "h-9 rounded-sm border bg-background px-2 text-sm";
  const refresh = () => qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: partsQuery().queryKey });
  const machineRecords = records
    .filter((r) => !form.machine_id || r.machine_id === form.machine_id)
    .sort((a, b) => b.performed_at.localeCompare(a.performed_at));

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!form.name.trim()) return;
    const rec = form.maintenance_id ? recById.get(form.maintenance_id) : undefined;
    const row = {
      name: form.name.trim(), quantity: Math.max(1, Number(form.quantity) || 1),
      machine_id: form.machine_id || rec?.machine_id || null, status: form.status, notes: form.notes,
      supplier: form.supplier.trim(), unit_price: form.unit_price === "" ? null : Number(form.unit_price.replace(",", ".")),
      purchased_at: form.purchased_at || null, maintenance_id: form.maintenance_id || null,
    };
    const { error } = editing
      ? await supabase.from("parts").update(row).eq("id", editing)
      : await supabase.from("parts").insert(row);
    if (error) { toast.error("Não foi possível salvar a peça."); return; }
    toast.success(editing ? "Peça atualizada." : "Peça registrada.");
    setForm(empty); setEditing(null); refresh();
  };
  const edit = (p: Part) => {
    setEditing(p.id);
    setForm({
      name: p.name, quantity: String(p.quantity), machine_id: p.machine_id ?? "", status: p.status as PartStatus,
      notes: p.notes, supplier: p.supplier, unit_price: p.unit_price == null ? "" : String(p.unit_price),
      purchased_at: p.purchased_at ?? "", maintenance_id: p.maintenance_id ?? "",
    });
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  };
  const setStatus = async (id: string, status: PartStatus) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("parts").update({ status }).eq("id", id);
    if (error) toast.error("Não foi possível atualizar."); else refresh();
  };
  const remove = async (id: string) => {
    const { error } = await supabase.from("parts").delete().eq("id", id);
    if (error) toast.error("Não foi possível excluir."); else refresh();
  };

  const shown = filterParts(parts, f);
  const count = (s: PartStatus) => shown.filter((p) => p.status === s).reduce((n, p) => n + p.quantity, 0);
  const toBuy = shown.filter((p) => p.status === "comprar");
  const bought = shown.filter((p) => p.status === "comprada");

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8">
      <div>
        <p className="label-tech">Peças</p>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Peças quebradas e compras</h1>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-5">
        {STATUSES.map((s) => (
          <button key={s} onClick={() => setF({ ...f, status: f.status === s ? "" : s })}
            className={`rounded-sm border bg-card p-4 text-left ${f.status === s ? "border-primary" : ""}`}>
            <p className={`font-mono-ui text-3xl font-bold ${PART_STATUS[s].cls}`}>{count(s)}</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{PART_STATUS[s].label}</p>
          </button>
        ))}
        <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4"><p className="font-mono-ui text-xl font-bold text-primary">{brl(partsCost(toBuy))}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Custo previsto (a comprar)</p></div>
        <div className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-4"><p className="font-mono-ui text-xl font-bold">{brl(partsCost(bought))}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Gasto com compradas</p></div>
      </div>

      <form onSubmit={save} className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <p className="label-tech basis-full">{editing ? "Editar peça" : "Registrar peça"}</p>
        <label className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Peça<Input required value={form.name} placeholder="Ex.: Rolamento 6205" onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="w-20 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Qtd.<Input type="number" min={1} value={form.quantity} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, quantity: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Máquina
          <select className={sel} value={form.machine_id} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, machine_id: e.target.value, maintenance_id: "" })}>
            <option value="">Nenhuma</option>
            {machines.map((m) => <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.name}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Situação
          <select className={sel} value={form.status} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, status: e.target.value as PartStatus })}>
            {STATUSES.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{PART_STATUS[s].label}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <label className="min-w-40 flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Fornecedor<Input value={form.supplier} placeholder="Ex.: SKF Distribuidora" onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, supplier: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="w-32 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Preço unitário (R$)<Input inputMode="decimal" value={form.unit_price} placeholder="0,00" onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, unit_price: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Data da compra<Input type="date" value={form.purchased_at} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, purchased_at: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Substituída na manutenção
          <select className={`${sel} max-w-72`} value={form.maintenance_id} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, maintenance_id: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Nenhuma</option>
            {machineRecords.map((r) => (
              <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{br(r.performed_at)} · {r.kind}{form.machine_id ? "" : ` · ${names.get(r.machine_id) ?? "Máquina"}`}{r.description ? ` · ${r.description.slice(0, 30)}` : ""}</option>
            ))}
          </select></label>
        <label className="basis-full text-xs text-muted-foreground">Observações<Input value={form.notes} placeholder="Ex.: motivo da quebra" onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, notes: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <Button type="submit">{editing ? "Salvar alterações" : "Registrar peça"}</Button>
        {editing && <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => { setEditing(null); setForm(empty); }}>Cancelar</Button>}
      </form>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3 rounded-sm border bg-card p-4">
        <label className="min-w-48 flex-1 text-xs text-muted-foreground">Buscar<Input value={f.term} placeholder="Nome, fornecedor ou observação" onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, term: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Máquina
          <select className={sel} value={f.machineId} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, machineId: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Todas</option>
            {machines.map((m) => <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>{m.name}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <label className="flex flex-col text-xs text-muted-foreground">Situação
          <select className={sel} value={f.status} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, status: e.target.value })}>
            <option value="">Todas</option>
            {STATUSES.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{PART_STATUS[s].label}</option>)}
          </select></label>
        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Registrada de<Input type="date" value={f.from} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, from: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <label className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Até<Input type="date" value={f.to} onChange={(e) => setF({ ...f, to: e.target.value })} /></label>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={() => setF({ term: "", machineId: "", status: "", from: "", to: "" })}>Limpar</Button>
      </div>

      {shown.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="rounded-sm border bg-card p-6 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
          {parts.length ? "Nenhuma peça encontrada com esses filtros." : "Nenhuma peça registrada."}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <ul className="divide-y rounded-sm border bg-card">
          {shown.map((p) => {
            const rec = p.maintenance_id ? recById.get(p.maintenance_id) : undefined;
            const price = p.unit_price == null ? null : Number(p.unit_price);
            return (
              <li key={p.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-4">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-0.5">
                  <p className="font-semibold">{p.quantity}× {p.name}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {p.machine_id ? names.get(p.machine_id) ?? "Máquina" : "Sem máquina"} · registrada em {br(p.created_at)}
                    {p.supplier ? ` · ${p.supplier}` : ""}
                    {price != null ? ` · ${brl(price)} un. (total ${brl(price * p.quantity)})` : ""}
                    {p.purchased_at ? ` · comprada em ${br(p.purchased_at)}` : ""}
                    {p.notes ? ` · ${p.notes}` : ""}
                  </p>
                  {rec && (
                    <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-success">
                      <Wrench className="size-3" /> Substituída na manutenção {rec.kind} de {br(rec.performed_at)}{rec.technician ? ` · ${rec.technician}` : ""}
                    </p>
                  )}
                </div>
                <select className={`${sel} ${PART_STATUS[p.status as PartStatus]?.cls ?? ""}`} value={p.status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(p.id, e.target.value as PartStatus)}>
                  {STATUSES.map((s) => <option key={s} value={s}>{PART_STATUS[s].label}</option>)}
                </select>
                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" aria-label="Editar peça" onClick={() => edit(p)}><Pencil /></Button>
                <Button size="icon" variant="ghost" aria-label="Excluir peça" onClick={() => remove(p.id)}><Trash2 /></Button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/routes/_authenticated/route.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { createFileRoute, Outlet, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { AppHeader } from "@/components/AppHeader";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/_authenticated")({
  ssr: false,
  beforeLoad: async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
    if (!data.user) throw redirect({ to: "/auth" });
    return { user: data.user };
  },
  component: () => (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-blueprint">
      <AppHeader />
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
  ),
});

ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/src/test/app-routing.test.tsx
####################################################################################################

import { QueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createRouter, rootRouteId } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { routeTree } from "@/routeTree.gen";

// Match routes without running loaders or rendering: loaders may need a server or
// network the test run lacks, and jsdom never loads the stylesheets React waits on.
describe("App routing", () => {
  it("matches a page for / instead of falling back to not found", () => {
    const router = createRouter({ routeTree, context: { queryClient: new QueryClient() } });

    const matches = router.matchRoutes("/");

    expect(matches.at(-1)?.routeId).not.toBe(rootRouteId);
  });
});

####################################################################################################
ARQUIVO: maqsense-src/supabase/config.toml

project_id = "gqcneyywrkdemxrauryg"
